Method: 方法为multi-scale、training-free、saliency-based、bottom-up注意力模型，直接作用于低分辨率事件输入；在多个downscaling factor下评估，输入分辨率相对全分辨率最多降低256倍；在事件相机数据集Prophesee Automotive上测试ROI选择。

Result: 在Prophesee Automotive上，不同尺度下ROI选择稳健，可检测多类目标（多种车辆、行人、交通灯、交通标志），准确率最高70.8%，时间分辨率达毫秒级，比数据集ground truth时间分辨率细16倍。

Conclusion: 早期事件降采样结合基于显著性的注意力可作为高效边缘神经形态视觉系统的有效前端，提升带宽与能效表现，但分类准确率仍有限。

Abstract: Neuromorphic vision systems operate under strict constraints on bandwidth, memory, and energy, particularly at the edge, motivating early mechanisms for data reduction and selective processing. In this work, we investigate a multi-scale training-free, saliency-based, bottom-up visual attention model that operates directly on low-resolution event-based input and selects Regions of Interest (ROI) from the visual scene. The model is evaluated across multiple downscaling factors applied to the incoming event stream, with input resolutions reduced by up to 256x relative to full resolution. Performance is assessed on the Prophesee Automotive dataset, the largest publicly available event-based dataset, demonstrating robust ROI selection across different scales on a real-world use-case. The proposed approach is capable of detecting ROIs belonging to multiple object classes, including various vehicle types, pedestrians, traffic lights, and traffic signs, with accuracy up to 70.8%, while operating at millisecond temporal resolution, 16x finer than the temporal resolution provided by the dataset ground truth. These results highlight the potential of combining early event downscaling with saliency-based attention as an effective front-end for efficient edge neuromorphic vision systems.

</details>


### [73] [From Foundation Embeddings to Cropland Maps: Label Efficiency, Temporal Transferability and Independent Human Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17138)
*Mohammad Ammar Mughees,Giovanni Montefoschi,Zhongxin Chen,Maria Antonia Brovelli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 评估冻结的AlphaEarth地理空间基础模型嵌入在美国缅因州进行耕地/非耕地二分类制图的可行性：轻量分类器在留出区块上达到93.7%总体精度，跨年份迁移稳定，并在385点人工共识验证上优于CDL参考产品（95.3% vs 91.7%），与微调TerraMind分割模型无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间基础模型被认为可提供可复用的卫星影像表征，减少下游任务建模成本，但其在区域尺度、跨年份与相对参考产品/微调模型的实际效用缺乏严格实证，尤其是"免微调冻结嵌入"是否足够。

Method: 使用年度AlphaEarth嵌入，在缅因州192个空间分离区块上做耕地/非耕地二分类，标签来自USDA CDL；不微调基础模型，比较逻辑回归、梯度提升集成、最近类质心规则；测试标签样本量效率（6万像素 vs 860万像素）；做2018–2023同区域跨年份迁移；在2023年一个连续区块的385个随机点上与双标注者盲共识、CDL及微调TerraMind分割模型比较（McNemar检验）。

Result: 留出区块总体精度93.7%、平衡精度90.8%；逻辑回归与梯度提升相差0.3个百分点以内，无参数质心规则达90.2%；6万像素样本与全量860万像素相差1.3个百分点以内；跨年份分类器在2018–2023保持精度；对盲共识一致率95.3%（κ=0.82），高于CDL的91.7%（κ=0.72，p=0.0161）；与微调TerraMind差异不显著（95.3% vs 93.5%，p=0.14）。

Conclusion: 冻结地理空间嵌入是以低计算成本进行区域耕地制图的有力候选，可能部分平滑了CDL标签噪声，但结果受限于单一州、30 m派生训练参考与单区块人工验证，不能据此声称对参考产品实现全州范围校正。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models provide reusable representations of satellite imagery that support downstream mapping with limited task-specific modelling. We evaluate whether annual AlphaEarth embeddings support binary cultivated-versus-non-cultivated mapping in Maine, USA, using 192 spatially separated patches and labels derived from the USDA Cropland Data Layer (CDL). Without fine-tuning the foundation model, a lightweight classifier reaches 93.7% overall accuracy and 90.8% balanced accuracy on held-out patches. Logistic regression is within 0.3 percentage points of a gradient-boosted ensemble, while a nearest-class-centroid rule, which uses class centroids but fits no parameters, reaches 90.2%. A balanced sample of 60,000 labelled pixels is within 1.3 percentage points of the full pool of 8.6 million pixels; because pixels are spatially autocorrelated, this result concerns pixel-sample efficiency rather than 60,000 independent annotation sites. In a same-region transfer experiment, classifiers trained in one year remain accurate across 2018 to 2023. Against a blind, two-interpreter consensus at 385 randomly sampled points in one contiguous 2023 block, the AlphaEarth-plus-random-forest map agrees at 95.3% ($\kappa=0.82$), compared with 91.7% for the CDL ($\kappa=0.72$; exact two-sided McNemar $p=0.0161$). This local result is consistent with partial smoothing of CDL label noise, but it does not establish statewide correction of the reference product. On the same points, the difference from a fine-tuned TerraMind segmentation model is not statistically significant (95.3% versus 93.5%; $p=0.14$), and the experiment is not a controlled comparison of computational cost. These results support frozen geospatial embeddings as a low-compute candidate for regional cropland mapping, subject to the limits of a single-state study, a 30 m-derived training reference, and a one-block human validation.

</details>


### [74] [ResLRP: The Role of Residual Cancellation in Attribution Instability in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17152)
*Jim Berend,Reduan Achtibat,Daniel Schäffer,Alexander Binder,Wojciech Samek,Sebastian Lapuschkin,Maximilian Dreyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ResLRP，通过显式处理残差连接中的抵消效应，解决 ViT 归因爆炸问题，并给出保守性与爆炸界限的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT 已成为主流视觉模型，但细粒度、忠实且稳定的输入归因仍困难。LRP 迁移到 Transformer 注意力后在 ViT 上产生噪声大、不忠实的解释。作者指出被忽视的关键是残差连接的处理：残差路径中的抵消效应导致归因爆炸，且该现象在 ViT 中比语言 Transformer 更强。

Method: 提出 Residual-aware Layer-wise Relevance Propagation（ResLRP）：对标准 LRP 的简单扩展，其传播规则显式建模残差分支中的抵消，保证严格保守性，并可证明地约束相关性的爆炸。通过因果的通道级干预实验验证响应机制。在监督、自监督、对比、层级与多模态 ViT 家族以及 FunnyBirds 基准上评估归因忠实度与定位质量，并用于在输入空间定位 SAE 特征。

Result: 归因质量在忠实度与定位两方面显著提升；现代 VLM 收益最大，定位提升 27-29%，忠实度最高提升 3.4 倍。因果干预确认不稳定由残差抵消而非泛化正则引起。残差放大度量可作为预测归因退化的架构级诊断指标。

Conclusion: 残差抵消是 ViT 归因不稳的核心原因，ResLRP 以最小改动提供保守且稳定可证的归因，并兼具诊断与 SAE 特征定位等下游用途。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) are central to most modern vision models, yet obtaining input attributions that are fine-grained, faithful, and stable remains challenging. Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP) has been adapted to transformer attention, but in ViTs it often produces noisy, unfaithful explanations. We show that the missing ingredient is the treatment of residual connections: cancellation effects in residual pathways lead to attribution explosion. Moreover, we find that these cancellations are substantially stronger in ViTs than in language transformers. To address this issue, we introduce Residual-aware Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (ResLRP), a simple extension of LRP whose propagation rules explicitly account for cancellations in residual branches, are exactly conservative, and provably bound relevance explosion. Causal channel-wise interventions confirm that residual cancellation, not a generic regularization effect, drives the instability. ResLRP substantially improves attribution quality across faithfulness and localization, evaluated on ViT architectures spanning supervised, self-supervised, contrastive, hierarchical, and multimodal families, as well as on the ground-truth-controlled FunnyBirds benchmark. The largest gains arise in modern Vision Language Models (VLMs), with +27-29% localization and up to 3.4x faithfulness scores. Beyond benchmarks, ResLRP localizes Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) features in input space, and our residual amplification measure serves as an architecture-level diagnostic predicting where attribution degrades.

</details>


### [75] [MUMINS: Metadata-conditioned Uncertainty-aware Medical Image Next-state Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17169)
*Anna Oliveras,Roger Marí,Rafael Redondo,Oriol Guardià,Cynthia Ifeyinwa Ugwu,Ana Tost,Bhalaji Nagarajan,Carolina Migliorelli,Vicent Ribas,Petia Radeva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MUMINS：元数据条件化、不确定性感知的医学图像下一状态合成扩散框架，单次反向扩散同时生成随访影像与空间不确定性图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解剖形态演化（肿瘤生长、神经退行）预测属生成式视觉难题：变化相对静态解剖极细微、高度个体化且本质随机。确定性网络忽略生物随机性，标准扩散模型需多趟采样才能量化不确定性，计算代价过高。

Method: 将基线扫描与其随访残差联合扩散、相加得到随访影像；在单次反向扩散中同步预测空间不确定性图。以时间间隔与相关元数据为条件，每步去噪动态重注入基线作为软锚点以保留细粒度解剖；用负对数似然头学习不确定性图，显式标注易错区域。不含器官特定先验，同一架构经数据集特定重训练复用于不同解剖部位。

Result: 在肺CT（PNG）与脑MRI（OASIS-3）上，经数据集特定重训练的MUMINS性能匹配或超越各领域的专用SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 元数据条件化+基线软锚点+单程不确定性建模可在不依赖器官启发式的前提下，跨解剖部位达到或超过专用SOTA，兼顾效率与不确定性量化。

Abstract: Forecasting anatomical changes such as tumor growth and neurodegeneration is a challenging generative vision task. Morphological evolution is subtle relative to static anatomy, highly patient-specific, and inherently stochastic. Existing methods struggle with several issues: deterministic networks ignore biological stochasticity, while standard diffusion models require computationally prohibitive multi-pass sampling to quantify uncertainty. We propose MUMINS (Metadata-conditioned Uncertainty-aware Medical Image Next-state Synthesis), an efficient diffusion framework that jointly diffuses a baseline scan and its follow-up residual, summed to synthesize the follow-up scan, while concurrently predicting a spatial uncertainty map, in a single reverse diffusion process. Conditioned on the time interval and relevant metadata, it preserves fine-grained anatomy by dynamically re-injecting the baseline as a soft anchor at every denoising step, and a negative-log-likelihood head learns the uncertainty map to explicitly flag error-prone regions. Designed without organ-specific heuristics, the same architecture is reused across anatomies via separate, dataset-specific retraining. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that dataset-specific retraining of MUMINS matches or outperforms dedicated, domain-specific state-of-the-art methods on lung CT (PNG) and brain MRI (OASIS-3). Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [76] [Multimodal Cultural Heritage Architectural Style Classification for Residential Buildings in the UAE Based on CLIP Embeddings and SVM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17181)
*Ahmed Ammar Kubba,Manar Abu Talib,Iman Ibrahim,Qassim Nasir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CLIP多模态框架（图像+专家文本融合为512维嵌入，UMAP降维+K-Means聚类，再以聚类标签训练SVM），对阿联酋住宅建筑风格实现8类98%分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑风格图像复杂，传统方法过度依赖CNN视觉特征而忽视文本描述，且缺乏非西方（中东）地区专属数据集，导致文化遗产建筑风格分析分类困难。

Method: 使用OpenAI CLIP模型提取建筑图像的视觉特征与专家描述文本特征，融合为统一512维嵌入；用UMAP降维可视化，K-Means无监督聚类；对聚类结果人工分析得到风格标签，据此训练SVM分类器完成自动风格分类。

Result: 在识别出的8个风格聚类上达到98%分类准确率，高于文献中其他同类研究，验证了视觉与文本模态结合的有效性。

Conclusion: 多模态AI可有效支撑建筑遗产分析与地域建筑身份探索，提供可扩展、可解释的工具；但方法为现成模型拼接，缺乏核心具身/三维重建/机器人等前沿要素。

Abstract: The analysis and classification of cultural heritage architectural styles remain challenging due to the complexity of visual images of buildings, which are highly relied on in traditional CNN-based classification approaches in comparison to textual descriptions, and the relative lack of non-western region-specific datasets. This paper addresses this gap by proposing a multimodal machine learning framework to analyze and classify Emirati residential architecture using OpenAI's CLIP model. We integrate visual features from images and textual features from expert descriptions into a unified 512-dimensional embedding, followed by dimensionality reduction with UMAP for visualization and unsupervised clustering using K-Means. Cluster labels, which are derived from manual analysis of the K-Means clusters, are used to train an SVM classifier for automated architectural style classification. Our approach achieves a classification accuracy of 98% across eight identified style clusters, higher than every other study in the literature, demonstrating the effectiveness of combining visual and textual modalities. Overall, this paper highlights the potential of using multimodal AI to support architectural heritage analysis, offering scalable and interpretable tools for exploring regional architectural identities.

</details>


### [77] [EventEgoHands++: Event-based Egocentric 3D Hand Mesh Reconstruction with Real Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17189)
*Ryosei Hara,Wataru Ikeda,Masashi Hatano,Mariko Isogawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EventEgoHands++ 提出以事件相机实现第一视角3D手部网格重建，通过实例级手部检测器区分左右手，并用自适应注意力门控建模双手空间关系与交互，同时构建约100万帧的真实数据集EEH-R。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相机在低光与严重运动模糊下失效，事件相机具备高动态范围与高时间分辨率，适合第一视角手部重建。但相机佩戴者自身运动产生密集背景事件，掩盖手部信号；此前首个第一视角事件方法仅用二值手部掩码缓解该问题，无法区分左右手，缺少实例级信息，导致在只有一只手或无手时仍预测双手，产生错误的双手关系与精度下降。

Method: 1）设计Hand Detector，估计左右手的实例级边界框与掩码，提供实例级手部信息；2）提出Adaptive Attention，基于检测结果动态门控注意力，准确学习双手的空间关系与相互交互；3）在合成N-HOT3D数据集基础上进行扩展，并新建EEH-R真实世界第一视角事件手部数据集，约100万带标注帧，包含低光环境。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法持续优于基线方法；EEH-R为迄今最大的真实事件第一视角手部数据集。

Conclusion: 通过实例级手部检测与自适应注意力门控，显式建模左右手实例信息与交互关系，可有效克服事件相机第一视角场景中背景事件干扰与双手歧义问题，提升3D手部网格重建精度；配套的大规模真实数据集为该方向提供了评测基础。

Abstract: 3D hand mesh reconstruction is a challenging yet essential task for downstream applications, including human-robot interaction and AR/VR. Although conventional cameras have been widely adopted for this task, methods that rely on them struggle in low-light environments and under severe motion blur. To address these limitations, event-based cameras have recently attracted attention for their high dynamic range and high temporal resolution. However, applying event cameras to egocentric hand reconstruction remains challenging because camera wearer's motion produces dense background events that obscure hand-specific signals. Although the first egocentric event-based approach mitigates this issue using hand segmentation, its binary hand mask does not distinguish between left and right hands. As a result, the model lacks instance-level hand information and predicts both hands even when only one or neither hand is present. This limitation leads to incorrect inter-hand relationships and degraded reconstruction accuracy. In this paper, we propose EventEgoHands++, a framework for event-based 3D hand mesh reconstruction from an egocentric viewpoint. The proposed method incorporates a Hand Detector that estimates instance-level bounding boxes and masks for both the left and right hands. Moreover, we introduce Adaptive Attention, which dynamically gates the attention based on these detection results to accurately learn the spatial relationship and mutual interactions between the hands. To train and evaluate our framework, we extend the synthetic N-HOT3D dataset and newly construct EEH-R, the largest real-world event-based egocentric hand dataset to date, comprising approximately 1M annotated frames captured in environments including low-light conditions. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real datasets demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms the baselines.

</details>


### [78] [Probe-VAD: Ordinal Likelihood Probing for Training-Free Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17211)
*Jiawei Gu,Qilin Zhao,Tengkuo Guo,Zhiming Zhong,Shuangqing Zhang,Fan Lyu,Fang Zhao,Guo-Sen Xie,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Probe-VAD 用冻结的视觉语言模型做序数二分类探针，直接输出连续、可排序的异常分数，实现免训练视频异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 VLM 的免训练 VAD 在"视觉理解"与"异常打分"之间的接口过于受限：字幕式流程把视觉证据压缩为文本，丢失细微线索；直接数值生成则强迫模型只用少量预定义分值表达判断。这些接口难以刻画异常严重程度的细微差异，导致视觉上差异明显的片段获得相似表征或分数，限制了异常排序的分辨率。

Method: 提出 Probe-VAD 序数二分类探针框架：对原始视频片段，向冻结 VLM 查询十个有序的严重度阈值，提取受约束的 YES/NO 续写似然；将其归一化为累积严重度剖面，从尾部证据聚合出连续异常分数，并用保序（isotonic）投影保证序数一致性。全程无需任务特定训练，也不依赖字幕压缩。

Result: 在公开 VAD 基准上取得更优性能，且计算开销低。

Conclusion: Probe-VAD 为把冻结 VLM 的视觉理解转化为连续、对排序敏感的异常分数提供了一种简单接口，无需训练与字幕压缩，代码已开源。

Abstract: Video anomaly detection (VAD) aims to localize anomalous events in untrimmed videos. Vision-language models (VLMs) provide rich visual understanding for training-free VAD, but existing approaches impose restrictive interfaces between visual understanding and anomaly scoring. Caption-based pipelines compress visual evidence into text, potentially discarding subtle cues, while direct numerical generation forces the model to express its judgment through a small set of predefined scores. Such interfaces can obscure subtle differences in anomaly severity, causing visually distinct clips to receive similar representations or scores and thereby limiting the resolution of anomaly ranking. We propose \textbf{Probe-VAD}, an ordinal binary-probing framework that directly probes severity preferences from a frozen VLM. Given raw video clips, Probe-VAD queries ten ordered severity thresholds and extracts constrained \textit{YES}/\textit{NO} continuation likelihoods. Their normalized preferences form a cumulative severity profile, from which tail evidence is aggregated into a continuous anomaly score, with isotonic projection enforcing ordinal consistency. Experiments on public VAD benchmarks demonstrate superior performance with low computational cost. Probe-VAD provides a simple interface for translating frozen VLM visual understanding into continuous, rank-sensitive anomaly scores without task-specific training or caption-based compression. Code is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [79] [InfoTaxa: Information-Calibrated Label-Free Clustering for Fine-Grained Visual Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17218)
*David Ahmedt-Aristizabal,Mohammad Ali Armin,Lars Petersson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作在 BIOSCAN‑5M 上对冻结视觉嵌入的无标签层次聚类进行信息校准分析：BioCLIP‑2+UMAP+HDBSCAN 在科级达 0.79 AMI、属级 0.67；并提出 InfoTaxa 用"聚类效率"量化无监督划分恢复的探针估计图像信息，以配对 DNA 仅作审计信号，发现物种级聚类受限既来自聚类方法也来自表征本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无标签聚类是生物多样性监测的可扩展路径，但纯图像细粒度分类存在稳定的"由粗到细"失效模式：聚类能恢复宽泛的分类结构却在物种级停滞。作者希望判定这种停滞是方法所限（聚类算法不足）还是信息所限（图像嵌入本身不含足够物种判别信息）。

Method: 在 BIOSCAN‑5M 上使用 BioCLIP‑2 冻结特征，配合 UMAP 降维与 HDBSCAN 密度聚类，并与 oracle‑K、图方法及学习式聚类头对比；提出 InfoTaxa 指标——聚类效率，即无监督划分所恢复的、由探针估计的图像信息比例；配对 DNA 仅作为审计信号而非推理输入；另用留出数据的后期融合探针量化添加 DNA 后物种级预测误差的下降；并做多种编码器、描述物种/稀有类子集、探针诊断、留出物种粗粒度泛化与同物种检索的稳健性分析。

Result: 密度聚类管线在目级与科级分别恢复约 0.90 与 0.81 的图像可用信息；科级 AMI 0.79、属级 0.67，显著优于先前图像基线，并与 oracle‑K、图方法、学习式聚类头在相同冻结特征上具有竞争力。后期融合探针表明，向图像嵌入中加入 DNA 可使物种级预测误差降低约 2 bit。

Conclusion: 在所测试设定下，物种级无标签聚类既受聚类方法限制也受表征限制：改进聚类或可恢复更多图像已暴露的结构，但无法仅凭聚类弥合经 DNA 审计出的信息鸿沟。

Abstract: Label-free clustering of frozen pretrained visual embeddings offers a scalable route to biodiversity monitoring, but image-only fine-grained taxonomy exhibits a consistent coarse-to-fine failure mode: clusters recover broad taxonomic structure yet plateau at species level. We study this behaviour on BIOSCAN-5M through an information-calibrated clustering analysis. BioCLIP~2 features with UMAP and HDBSCAN reach $0.79$ AMI at family and $0.67$ at genus, substantially improving over the prior image baseline and remaining competitive with oracle-$K$, graph-based, and learned clustering heads on the same frozen features. To diagnose whether the remaining plateau is method-limited or information-limited, we introduce InfoTaxa, which combines clustering efficiency---the fraction of probe-estimated image information recovered by an unsupervised partition---with paired DNA as an audit signal only, not an inference input. The density pipeline recovers approximately $0.90$ and $0.81$ of the image-available information at order and family, respectively. Held-out late-fusion probes show that adding DNA to the image embedding reduces species-level prediction error by approximately two bits. Robustness analyses cover multiple image encoders, described-species and rare-class subsets, probe diagnostics, and held-out-species coarse-rank generalisation and same-species retrieval. Thus, in the tested setting, species-level label-free clustering is both clustering-limited and representation-limited: improved clustering may recover additional image-exposed structure, but cannot close the DNA-audited information gap alone.

</details>


### [80] [FROD: Feature Matching Residual Denoising Oracle Bone Decipher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17227)
*Yanbin Hou,Biao Xiong,Guojun Xu,Jianwen Xiang,Cheng Tan,Yanchao Yang,Junwei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FROD框架，将甲骨文破译辅助建模为跨时代图像翻译任务，结合快速特征匹配的门控分割监督、残差去噪扩散模型（RDDM）与多阶段字体风格化精修网络，在字符不相交数据集上Top-1识别准确率比OBSD高3.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲骨文破译高度依赖领域专家人工分析语义语境与字形演变，过程劳动密集。自动辅助破译的核心难点在于甲骨文字形与现代汉字差异巨大，但其部首层面常保留局部拓扑不变量，同时常规扩散模型易产生位置漂移与笔画紊乱。

Method: 构造跨时代图像翻译任务；训练时用快速特征匹配生成门控分割监督——匹配度高的样本对按patch分块对齐细粒度部首，低相似度样本整体训练以避免错配伪影；引入残差去噪扩散模型（RDDM）同时估计噪声与残差信号以抑制位置漂移和笔画紊乱；最后用多阶段字体风格化精修网络去除边缘噪声、稳定笔画结构。

Result: 在扩充后的字符不相交数据集上，FROD的Top-1识别准确率超过所评估的基线方法，相较OBSD取得3.8%的绝对提升。

Conclusion: 将甲骨文破译辅助形式化为跨时代图像翻译，并以特征匹配门控监督与残差扩散建模相结合，可在字符不相交设定下提升古文字识别性能，为专家破译提供辅助工具。

Abstract: Oracle bone script (OBS), one of the earliest Chinese writing systems, plays an important role in the study of Chinese etymology. Traditional decipherment relies heavily on domain experts who analyze characters through semantic context and structural evolution. To assist this labor-intensive process, we formulate OBS decipherment assistance as a cross-era image translation task and propose FROD (Feature Matching Residual Denoising Oracle Bone Decipher). Although many OBS characters differ substantially from their modern counterparts, they often preserve local topological invariants at the radical level. During training, FROD leverages fast feature matching to provide gated segmentation supervision: paired samples with sufficient matches are processed patch-wise to align fine-grained radicals, whereas low-similarity pairs are trained holistically to avoid mismatched artifacts. In addition, a Residual Denoising Diffusion Model (RDDM) jointly estimates noise and residual signals, thereby reducing the positional drift and stroke disorder commonly observed in standard diffusion models. Finally, a multi-stage font stylization refinement network refines the generated images by eliminating edge noise and stabilizing stroke structures. On our augmented character-disjoint dataset, FROD achieves higher Top-1 recognition accuracy than the evaluated baselines, with a 3.8% absolute gain over OBSD.

</details>


### [81] [DecoGS: Adaptive Static-Dynamic Decoupling of 3D Gaussians for Free-Viewpoint Video Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17230)
*Idil Sulo,Alexey Supikov,Ilke Demir,Sainan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DecoGS：流式视频在线训练3D高斯时，用自适应机制只在有运动或光度变化的时空区域做优化，并配合梯度门控与可见性过滤管理高斯，实现高速、低闪烁的在线重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式3D重建方法每帧更新全部高斯，即使在静态区域也做冗余优化，导致闪烁、漂移与效率低下。作者希望在保持时间一致性与高保真度的同时大幅提升速度并压缩内存。

Method: DecoGS面向流式视频的3D高斯在线训练，核心是自适应选择性优化：仅对出现运动或光度变化的时空区域进行优化，避开静态区域的冗余更新；并引入区域感知的高斯管理，包括梯度门控（gradient gating）与高效可见性过滤（visibility filtering），以维持时间连贯性并控制内存规模；无需大规模预训练。

Result: 在N3DV与MeetRoom数据集上分别达到34.55 dB和31.60 dB PSNR，优于所有流式与离线基线；渲染速度261 FPS；时间闪烁比最优先前方法低70倍；无需大规模预训练。

Conclusion: 选择性、区域感知的优化是流式动态3D高斯重建兼顾速度、保真度与时间稳定性的有效途径，DecoGS在精度、实时性与闪烁抑制上均取得领先。

Abstract: Streaming 3D reconstruction demands both speed and temporal fidelity, goals that existing methods undermine by updating every Gaussian every frame, even in static regions. We present DecoGS, a method for efficient online training of 3D Gaussians from streaming videos. Unlike prior methods that update the entire scene indiscriminately, DecoGS introduces an adaptive mechanism that selectively focuses optimization on spatiotemporal regions exhibiting motion or photometric changes. This targeted training strategy eliminates redundant updates that cause flickering and drift in nominally static regions, while enabling fast, high-fidelity scene updates. The pipeline further integrates region-aware Gaussian management through gradient gating and efficient visibility filtering to maintain temporal coherence and a compact memory footprint. On N3DV and MeetRoom, DecoGS achieves 34.55 and 31.60 dB PSNR respectively, outperforming all streaming and offline baselines, while rendering at 261 FPS with $70\times$ lower temporal flicker than the best prior method, requiring no large-scale pretraining.

</details>


### [82] [Video-HolmesV2: Can MLLMs Reason with Spatio-Temporal Audio-Visual Evidence in Long Videos?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17248)
*Zhaoyang Wei,Zipeng Wang,Yushe Cao,Chenhui Qiang,Shuaibing Cheng,Xuesong Yang,Sen Nie,Bowen Jiang,Wenchao Ding,Yanchao Hao,Zheng Wei,Xuehui Yu,Zhenjun Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Video-HolmesV2 基准，强调音视频深度耦合与证据化评测，并给出音文引导的 token 压缩框架以缓解长视频长上下文噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型长视频叙事推理能力被视觉中心评测掩盖：基准过度依赖视觉启发式、边缘化听觉线索，模型退化为"沉默观察者"；同时稠密采样带来证据与上下文的权衡，帧数增加引发注意力分散与 token 爆炸。故需一个强制音视频跨模态推理且要求时空证据支撑的评测基准。

Method: 构建 Video-HolmesV2 基准，实行证据化评测（Evidence-Based Evaluation），要求模型以精确时空视听证据支撑答案；引入多模型交叉验证流水线保证任务严谨性，并设计时空证据感知度量进行细粒度校准；另提出音文引导的 token 压缩框架，融合任务意图与听觉锚点蒸馏高价值推理线索，抑制长上下文噪声。

Result: 评测中即使强闭源模型准确率也低于 60%；所提方法在同级开源全模态模型对比中表现更优。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了长视频音视频耦合推理的评测缺口，并以证据化评测与 token 压缩方法提供了可行的改进方向，说明细粒度跨模态证据对齐仍是当前模型的瓶颈。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models have demonstrated impressive video understanding, yet their ability to reason over long-form narratives is often masked by visual-centric evaluations and inefficient context processing. Existing benchmarks over-rely on visual heuristics while marginalizing auditory cues, effectively reducing models to "silent observers" that bypass genuine cross-modal reasoning. Moreover, standard dense sampling creates an evidence-context trade-off: increasing frames to capture evidence inevitably leads to attention distraction and token explosion. To bridge these gaps, we present Video-HolmesV2, a novel benchmark designed for Deep Audio-Visual Coupling. Unlike previous works, it enforces an Evidence-Based Evaluation, requiring models to justify answers with precise spatio-temporal audio-visual evidence, thereby reducing confounding effects of guessing and hallucinated evidence. To support this, we introduce: (1) a Multi-Model Cross-Verification pipeline to ensure task rigor; (2) a Spatio-temporal Evidence-Aware Metric for fine-grained calibration. Furthermore, we propose an Audio-Text Guided Token Compression framework. By fusing task intent with auditory anchors, our method distills high-value reasoning cues to mitigate long-context noise. In our evaluation, even strong proprietary models achieve below 60% accuracy, while our approach outperforms comparable open-source omni-models.

</details>


### [83] [Exploring 2D backbone effects for indoor semantic occupancy prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17257)
*Shizhang Fanga,Wanling Yea,Qi Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在固定EmbodiedScan的RGB-D投影、深度分支与占用头的前提下，仅替换2D图像主干，发现主干选择对语义占用预测精度的影响大于多种3D占用架构/模块设计：DINOv2达30.55% mIoU，BLIP2为29.49%，CLIP-ViT为24.33%，CLIP-ResNet仅17.41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RGB-D具身语义占用管线（如EmbodiedScan）中，图像编码器通常被当作默认模块，但其特征正是被采样进3D体素的视觉证据。作者希望直接研究这一被忽视的设计选择，验证2D主干是否是最终3D预测精度的主要变化来源。

Method: 控制变量实验：保持RGB-D投影、深度分支与占用头不变，仅替换2D图像主干，对比CLIP-ResNet、CLIP-ViT、BLIP2、DINOv2四种编码器，并给出总体mIoU与类别级结果对比。

Result: mIoU差异显著：DINOv2 30.55% > BLIP2 29.49% > CLIP-ViT 24.33% > CLIP-ResNet 17.41%；强主干无需改动下游3D融合管线即可超越原始EmbodiedScan ResNet-50基线。类别层面DINOv2在布局与结构类更强，BLIP2在部分物体中心类接近；CLIP-ViT明显优于CLIP-ResNet，说明CLIP特征作为稠密token的暴露方式对体素提升有影响。

Conclusion: 图像主干并非具身语义占用中的次要工程细节，而是最终3D预测变异的主要来源，值得作为独立研究方向予以系统评测。

Abstract: Semantic occupancy prediction gives an embodied agent a voxel-level account of where space is free, occupied, and semantically meaningful. In RGB-D pipelines such as EmbodiedScan, the image encoder is often left as a default module, even though its features are the visual evidence later sampled into the 3D grid. We study this design choice directly. A central finding is that changing the 2D backbone improves occupancy accuracy more than several carefully designed occupancy architectures or modules. We keep the main RGB-D projection, depth branch, and occupancy head fixed, and replace only the image backbone. The compared encoders are CLIP-ResNet, CLIP-ViT, BLIP2, and DINOv2. Under the controlled setting, the measured mIoU changes substantially: DINOv2 obtains 30.55\%, BLIP2 obtains 29.49\%, CLIP-ViT obtains 24.33\%, and CLIP-ResNet obtains 17.41\%. The stronger encoders also exceed the original EmbodiedScan ResNet-50 baseline without modifying the downstream 3D fusion pipeline. Class-level results give a more detailed picture: DINOv2 is stronger on many layout and structural categories, whereas BLIP2 remains close on several object-centered classes. CLIP-ViT improves clearly over CLIP-ResNet, showing that the way CLIP features are exposed as dense tokens matters for voxel lifting. These results indicate that the image backbone is not a secondary engineering detail in embodied semantic occupancy, but a major source of variation in the final 3D prediction.

</details>


### [84] [Semantic-Spatial Agreement Verification for Mitigating Object Hallucination in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17269)
*Ziheng Ren,Qian Gao,Jun Fan,Guohui Ding,Zhenyu Yang,Yuteng Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练方法SSAV，通过跨查询语义一致性与区域定位持续性来验证多模态大模型的对象声明，在POPE与CHAIR上显著降低物体幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在药物辅助、无障碍感知、环境决策等场景中可能"臆造"图中不存在的物体，构成现实安全风险，因此需要可靠的外部验证机制。

Method: SSAV无训练验证：多提示聚合估计语义支持度以降低措辞敏感；Query-Induced Regional Verification（QIRV）结合跨查询区域持续性、空间重叠度与相对候选优势，识别孤立高响应与分散定位；用几何平均融合语义与空间证据，任一分支缺支持即降分。

Result: 在3个基座模型与多种评测协议上有效抑制物体幻觉：LLaVA-1.5-7B在COCO/A-OKVQA/GQA平均准确率于POPE Popular与Adversarial下分别提升1.81、3.17个百分点，CHAIRs由49.40%降至32.80%。

Conclusion: 跨查询语义稳定性与区域一致性可为对象声明提供可解释的外部视觉证据，无需训练即可缓解幻觉。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models generate natural-language responses from visual inputs, yet may mention objects absent from an image. In medication assistance, accessible perception, and environmental decision-making, such hallucinations can create real-world safety risks. We propose Semantic-Spatial Agreement Verification (SSAV), a training-free method for verifying object claims. A visually grounded claim should remain stable across semantically equivalent queries and repeatedly localize to the same image region. SSAV aggregates multiple prompts to estimate semantic support and reduce sensitivity to query wording. Query-Induced Regional Verification (QIRV) combines cross-query region persistence, spatial overlap, and relative candidate dominance to identify isolated high responses and dispersed localizations. A geometric mean fuses semantic and spatial evidence, lowering the verification score when either branch lacks support. Experiments on three base models and multiple evaluation protocols show that SSAV effectively mitigates object hallucination. On LLaVA-1.5-7B, accuracy averaged across COCO, A-OKVQA, and GQA improves by 1.81 and 3.17 percentage points under POPE Popular and Adversarial, respectively, while CHAIRs decreases from 49.40% to 32.80%. These results show that cross-query semantic stability and regional consistency provide interpretable external visual evidence for object claims.

</details>


### [85] [Optical-Flow Wingbeat Counting in MuJoCo: A Comparison of Convolutional, Spiking, and Attention-Based Temporal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17308)
*Zhang Nengbo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在MuJoCo虚拟环境中用Crazyflie相机观测三种扑翼飞行器，比较三种时序架构从符号光流计数翅拍，各距离精确计数准确率约92%–97%，但场景bootstrap置信区间均含零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扑翼飞行器的视觉监测需要同时区分单次翅拍（运动强度）与平均频率，现有方法缺乏对因果、可部署的光流翅拍计数可行性的系统评估，也缺乏对计数指标（总数 vs 事件级）的联合报告意识。

Method: 受控MuJoCo评估：虚拟相机装在Crazyflie上，光距1.5 m与3.0 m，三种扑翼模型，240组配对场景配置生成1440个片段，场景级3:1训练-测试划分（防泄漏）。共享空间卷积编码器 + 三种因果时序头（因果时序卷积网络、循环LIF脉冲网络、因果自注意力），均预测相位与活跃度，接同一有向穿越事件计数器。保留6个已有卷积模型，12个新模型在生成测试预测前冻结权重。统计上用配对场景bootstrap。

Result: 1.5 m处三架构精确计数准确率为96.67%、95.00%、96.67%；3.0 m处为94.44%、92.22%、95.00%。所有配对场景bootstrap的精确计数准确率差值区间均包含零（即架构间无显著差异）。7个远距离脉冲模型片段总数正确但事件时序不匹配，说明总数与事件级指标须并报。误差呈边界敏感特征。

Conclusion: 结果支持该测试设定下因果光流翅拍计数的可行性，并识别出边界敏感误差；但不足以确立架构排名，也未验证重复训练稳定性、真实飞行鲁棒性与硬件效率。

Abstract: Visual monitoring of flapping-wing vehicles requires distinguishing individual wingbeats from motion strength and average frequency. This paper presents a controlled MuJoCo evaluation of wingbeat counting from signed optical flow observed by virtual cameras mounted on Crazyflie vehicles. Three flapping-wing models were recorded at optical distances of 1.5 and 3.0 m, producing 1,440 clips from 240 paired scene configurations with a scene-level 3:1 training-test split. A common spatial convolutional encoder was combined with a causal temporal convolutional network, a recurrent leaky integrate-and-fire spiking network, or causal self-attention. Each model predicted phase and activity, followed by the same directed-crossing event counter. The six existing convolutional models were retained, and all twelve new models were frozen before their test predictions were generated. Exact-count accuracies at 1.5 m were 96.67%, 95.00%, and 96.67%, respectively; at 3.0 m they were 94.44%, 92.22%, and 95.00%. All paired scene-bootstrap intervals for differences in exact-count accuracy included zero. Seven far-distance spiking-model clips had correct totals despite event-timing mismatches, demonstrating why total-count and event-level measurements must be reported together. The results support the feasibility of causal optical-flow counting in the tested setting and identify boundary-sensitive errors. They do not establish an architecture ranking across repeated training, real-flight robustness, or hardware efficiency.

</details>


### [86] [PanoGS-SLAM: Panoramic 3D Gaussian Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17387)
*Yongqi Mao,Hao Shi,Yufan Zhang,Zhonghua Yi,Xiangfei Guo,Kaiwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PanoGS-SLAM 是首个基于 3D Gaussian Splatting 的全景稠密 SLAM 系统，在球面域直接进行可微渲染与位姿优化，并引入球面一致光度损失与深度引导高斯初始化，在 PALVIO 与 SynPano 基准上取得跟踪精度与渲染质量的提升，且保持实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3DGS 类 SLAM 多面向窄视场针孔相机，角覆盖有限导致位姿可观测性弱，快速运动与大视角变化下光度优化不稳定。全景相机提供全向约束，有望提升跟踪稳定性与建图质量，但缺乏相应的稠密 GS-SLAM 方案。

Method: 在球面域进行可微渲染与位姿优化以实现全向光度约束；提出球面一致光度损失以补偿等距柱状投影的面积畸变；提出深度引导的高斯初始化策略以稳定新观测区域的增量建图。

Result: 在真实与合成全景基准（PALVIO、SynPano）上，跟踪精度与渲染质量一致优于几何类与 GS 类基线，前端收敛快且达到实时性能；受控视场实验显示角覆盖增大时优化条件与收敛稳定性呈单调改善。

Conclusion: 感知几何（视场角覆盖）从根本上塑造了基于可微高斯的 SLAM 的优化景观；全景 3DGS 稠密 SLAM 可行且有效，代码将开源。

Abstract: Real-time dense SLAM is a core capability for robotics applications that require robust localization and high- quality mapping in dynamic or fast-changing environments. Recent 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)-based SLAM methods have shown promising performance, but most are designed for narrow-FoV pinhole cameras, where limited angular coverage weakens pose observability and often leads to unstable photo- metric optimization under rapid motion and large viewpoint changes. We present PanoGS-SLAM, the first panoramic dense SLAM system built on 3D Gaussian Splatting. Our method per- forms differentiable rendering and pose optimization directly in the spherical domain, enabling omnidirectional photometric constraints for more stable tracking. To improve geometric consistency and robustness, we introduce (1) a sphere-consistent photometric loss that compensates for the area distortion of equirectangular projection, and (2) a depth-guided Gaussian initialization strategy that stabilizes incremental mapping in newly observed regions. Extensive experiments on both real and synthetic panoramic benchmarks (PALVIO and SynPano) show that PanoGS-SLAM consistently outperforms geometric and GS-based baselines in tracking accuracy and rendering quality, while achieving fast front-end convergence and real-time perfor- mance. In addition, controlled field-of-view experiments reveal a clear monotonic improvement in optimization conditioning and convergence stability as angular coverage increases, high- lighting the fundamental role of sensing geometry in shaping the optimization landscape of differentiable Gaussian-based SLAM. The source code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [87] [SSC-Priors: Exploring Semantic and Visibility Priors to Boost Lidar Semantic Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17413)
*Tetiana Martyniuk,Jonathan Seele,Alexandre Boulch,Gilles Puy,Renaud Marlet,Raoul de Charette*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文探索以低成本“先验注入”方式提升激光雷达语义场景补全（SSC）性能：将语义伪标签与传感器可见性信息作为额外输入，只需最小化架构改动，即可让旧模型在SemanticKITTI和SSCBench-nuScenes上接近甚至超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年SSC研究主要沿架构创新路线推进，模型越来越重、越来越复杂（如联合训练点云语义分割分支），但性能增益来源不清晰、成本高。作者希望回答：不重设计架构，仅通过更好的输入先验能否显著提升现有SSC网络？

Method: 提出两种简单先验作为附加输入：(1) 来自现成分割器的语义伪标签，直接拼接到输入点云；(2) 可见性信息，区分“射线间的空白空间”与“视线外的未知空间”。通过少量输入通道扩展适配原网络，系统研究可见性数据表示的设计空间，并用真值oracle界定剩余提升上限。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI上，两种先验使四类架构的较旧模型具备与SOTA竞争的能力，其中一种架构甚至超越SOTA；实验还表明高质量语义先验是mIoU提升的主要驱动，模型用真值语义训练一次后即可搭配任意分割器推理而无需重训。在SSCBench-nuScenes上，两种先验在更稀疏的32线传感器下同样可迁移。

Conclusion: 架构创新并非SSC性能提升的唯一途径；高质量语义先验与可见性先验是廉价且有效的“输入侧”增益来源，可即插即用地增强现有SSC模型并具跨数据集、跨传感器迁移性。

Abstract: This paper investigates easy strategies to boost the performance of existing networks for lidar semantic scene completion (SSC) without requiring complex architectural redesigns. The fact is that, over the last years, SSC methods have mostly pursued architectural innovations, making the models heavier and more complex, e.g., by jointly training a point cloud semantic segmentation branch. In this work, we take a step back and explore two priors used as simple ingredients (possibly noisy) to improve existing approaches: semantic pseudo-labels and sensor visibility information. Concretely, we provide both kinds of information directly as additional inputs to a given SSC network, requiring only a minimal adaptation of the original architecture. We first demonstrate that endowing input point clouds with semantic pseudo-labels from off-the-shelf segmenters significantly improves the performance of existing SSC models. In fact, by evaluating these models against an oracle, we establish that high-quality semantic priors are a primary driver of semantic gains (mIoU), and that the SSC model can be trained just once with ground-truth semantics and then exploited without retraining using any segmenter. Furthermore, we equip the input lidar point cloud with visibility information that distinguishes between empty spaces (between the lidar and a scanned point) and unknown spaces (outside of lines of sight), providing a secondary performance boost across the tested architectures. We study the design space of data for representing visibility information and bound the remaining headroom with a ground-truth oracle on the free-space labels. On SemanticKITTI, these enhancements make older models competitive with state-of-the-art systems across four architectures, in one case even outperforming them. On the SSCBench-nuScenes benchmark, both priors also transfer with the sparser 32-beam sensor.

</details>


### [88] [SlotDiT: Object-Centric Representations for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17414)
*Gjergj Plepi,Sven Behnke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SlotDiT 将文本引导的扩散 Transformer（DiT）从像素/VAE 潜空间迁移到以 slot 为核心的对象级潜空间，在统一 DiT 框架下对比 slot、VAE 与语义对齐三类潜表征，发现在四个机器人数据集上生成质量相当且任务完成率更高、计算更省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本条件潜扩散视频模型（如 DiT）依赖像素级或 VAE 潜表征，缺乏显式语义结构，潜空间设计对生成与下游机器人任务的影响尚未被系统研究；另一方面，对象级 slot 表征在动力学建模与规划中已被验证有效，但从未用于基于扩散的生成式建模。

Method: 提出 SlotDiT：输入参考图像与语言指令，将场景分解为表示单个实体的对象级 slot，在指令与场景上下文条件下自回归地对未来 slot 轨迹进行去噪，从而预测场景动态；并在统一的 DiT 框架下将 slot 表征与 VAE 表征、语义对齐表征进行受控对比。

Result: 在四个机器人数据集上，以 slot 作为 DiT 潜变量取得与 VAE/语义对齐方案相当的视频生成质量，同时一致提升任务完成率，且表征更紧凑、计算效率更高。

Conclusion: 对象级结构（object-centric structure）是机器人环境下扩散生成建模的一种强归纳偏置，slot 潜空间可作为兼具语义结构与计算效率的替代方案。

Abstract: Text-conditioned latent diffusion models perform strongly in video generation and are promising backbones for robotic applications. However, existing approaches rely on pixel-level or VAE-based latent representations that lack explicit semantic structure, leaving the impact of the representation space largely unexplored. Slot-based object-centric representations offer a structured alternative by decomposing scenes into object-level latents, or slots. While they have shown success in dynamics modeling and planning, they have not yet been explored for diffusion-based generative modeling. We introduce SlotDiT, a text-guided Diffusion Transformer (DiT) that operates in a slot-based latent space. Given a reference image and a language instruction, SlotDiT decomposes the scene into object-centric slots representing individual entities. Conditioned on the instruction and observed scene context, the model autoregressively denoises future slot trajectories to predict scene dynamics. To systematically investigate latent-space design for diffusion transformers, we compare slot-based representations against VAE-based and semantics-aligned alternatives within a unified DiT framework. Our experiments show that using slots as DiT latents yields competitive video generation quality while consistently improving task-completion rates across four robotic datasets. Furthermore, their compact representation provides a computationally efficient alternative to VAE-based and semantics-aligned latent spaces. Overall, our results demonstrate that object-centric structure is a powerful inductive bias for diffusion-based generative modeling in robotic environments. The project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [89] [Tracking the Unseen: An Occlusion-Robust Framework for Target Tracking Under Full and Long-Term Occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17427)
*Mais Mohammed,Sharifa Mohammed,Hanan Awadh,Haneen Bamaas,Raghad Bawazeer,Elham Alghamdi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种抗遮挡多目标跟踪框架，融合YOLOv11n检测、卡尔曼滤波运动预测与遮挡感知外观重识别（选用OAMN），在OVIS和自建军事数据集上相比OccluTrack显著提升MOTA与IDF1并减少ID切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多目标跟踪系统在完全遮挡与长时遮挡下易过早终止轨迹，导致身份丢失，影响国防、监控等场景的态势感知；需要一种能在目标消失于视野期间维持身份与轨迹连续性的跟踪框架。

Method: 三阶段框架：目标检测（YOLOv11n）、遮挡期间位置估计（卡尔曼滤波运动预测）、目标重现后的身份恢复（遮挡感知外观Re-ID）。在同一跟踪框架、相同条件下评测六种Re-ID架构，Occlusion-Aware Mask Network (OAMN) 综合表现最佳并入选最终流程。在公开OVIS数据集上对比OccluTrack，并在模拟长时遮挡的军事数据集上验证。

Result: OVIS数据集上MOTA相对提升18.1%、IDF1相对提升25.1%、身份切换减少12.8%；自建军事数据集上MOTA 0.734、IDF1 0.729，相对OccluTrack分别提升14.2%和5.8%。系统在困难遮挡条件下表现出良好的轨迹连续性、身份保持与轨迹估计可靠性。

Conclusion: 将检测、运动预测与遮挡感知Re-ID结合可有效维持长时遮挡下的身份与轨迹连续性，适用于需要持续目标跟踪的国防监控类应用；但方法为既有组件的集成式系统，缺乏机器人平台验证与开源细节。

Abstract: Real-time multi-object tracking systems remain highly vulnerable to full and long-term occlusion, where targets temporarily or completely disappear from the camera's field of view. Conventional trackers may terminate trajectories prematurely, resulting in identity loss and reduced situational awareness in applications such as defense and surveillance. This work proposes an occlusion-robust target tracking framework that maintains target identity and trajectory continuity through the integration of YOLOv11n object detection, Kalman Filter motion prediction, and occlusion-aware appearance-based re-identification. The framework consists of three stages: object detection, position estimation during occlusion, and identity recovery after target reappearance. Six Re-Identification (Re-ID) architectures were evaluated within the same tracking framework under identical conditions, with the Occlusion-Aware Mask Network (OAMN) achieving the best overall performance and therefore selected for the final pipeline. The framework was benchmarked against OccluTrack on the public OVIS dataset, achieving relative improvements of 18.1 percent in Multiple Object Tracking Accuracy (MOTA) and 25.1 percent in Identity F1 Score (IDF1), while reducing identity switches by 12.8 percent. On a custom military dataset simulating surveillance and battlefield-like environments with long-term occlusion, the framework achieved a MOTA of 0.734 and an IDF1 of 0.729, corresponding to relative improvements of 14.2 percent and 5.8 percent over OccluTrack. The system demonstrated strong tracking continuity, robust identity preservation, and reliable trajectory estimation under challenging occlusion conditions, highlighting its effectiveness for defense-related surveillance applications requiring continuous target tracking during visibility loss.

</details>


### [90] [BrainFocus: EEG-Guided ROI Selection for Efficient Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17443)
*Yihui Peng,Guorui Lu,Qinyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BrainFocus 提出用 EEG 信号预测目标语义类别、用 YOLO 定位 ROI,并在双置信度阈值通过时只把裁剪区域送入 VLM,否则回退全图;在自建 40 类 EEG-ImageNet 杂乱图像基准上,于 Qwen3.5-VL 2B/4B/9B 上提升 VQA 准确率 4.14–9.87 pp,同时减少 23%–39% 输入/total token 与 FLOPs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大尺寸杂乱图像的 VQA 计算开销高,而往往只有小区域相关。EEG 可提供人类来源的 ROI 语义线索,但 EEG 视觉类别解码本身不完美,直接用于 ROI 路由不可靠,因此需要一个对解码错误鲁棒的 EEG 引导高效推理框架。

Method: BrainFocus:EEG 分类器预测目标类别,YOLO 检测器定位对应 ROI;仅当两个预测都通过置信度阈值时,VLM 只处理裁剪 ROI,否则处理整图(双阈值门控 + 回退策略)。评估上基于 EEG-ImageNet 构建 40 类基准,含生成式杂乱图像与真实物体中心图像,带目标 ROI 标注和 600 组英文 VQA 对;在 Qwen3.5-VL 2B/4B/9B 上验证。

Result: 在杂乱场景中,VQA 准确率提升 4.14–9.87 pp;输入 token 与总 token 分别减少 23.2%–39.4% 与 23.2%–39.3%,端到端 FLOPs 减少 23.2%–39.5%。

Conclusion: 即使 EEG 语义解码不完美,EEG 信号仍可作为可靠的门控线索引导 VLM 的高效推理,实现准确率与计算成本的同步改善。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong visual question answering (VQA) performance, but processing large cluttered images is computationally expensive when only a small region is relevant. Electroencephalography (EEG) signals, which capture human neural responses to visual stimuli, can provide a human-derived semantic cue about the region of interest (ROI). However, EEG-guided visual category decoding remains imperfect, making direct ROI routing unreliable. In this work, we propose BrainFocus, a reliable EEG-guided efficient VLM framework for VQA. An EEG classifier predicts a target category, and a YOLO detector localizes the matching ROI. The VLM receives the cropped ROI only when both predictions pass confidence thresholds; otherwise, it processes the full image. For evaluation, we build on EEG-ImageNet to construct a 40-class benchmark comprising generated cluttered images and real object-centric images, with target-ROI annotations and 600 English visual question-answer pairs. Across Qwen3.5-VL 2B, 4B, and 9B models, BrainFocus improves VQA accuracy by 4.14-9.87 percentage points (pp) on cluttered scenes while reducing input tokens and total tokens by 23.2%-39.4% and 23.2%-39.3%, and end-to-end floating-point operations (FLOPs) by 23.2%-39.5%. These results demonstrate that EEG can guide efficient VLM inference even when its semantic decoding is imperfect.

</details>


### [91] [ORCA: Occlusion-Aware Refinement and Completion for Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17450)
*Weronika Jakubowska,Maciej Zięba,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ORCA提出遮挡感知的单图重建框架，在Gaussian-anchor表示中区分小遮挡与大缺失：小缝隙用已有RGB-D信息修补，仅大区域才用生成式inpainting，从而减少幻觉并提升新视角质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图新视角合成本质欠定，相机远离输入视角后会出现遮挡区域露出、几何缺失与空洞。现有方法普遍依赖生成模型补全，但许多伪影只是深度边界附近的小缺口，并不需要生成新内容，白白引入生成幻觉。

Method: ORCA将单目深度引入Gaussian-anchor表示并保持原始相机射线对应关系；探索新视角时按缺失区域的尺寸与结构分类处理：小范围unocclusion用重建中已有的RGB-D信息修复，仅对大范围且无法从场景可靠恢复的区域才调用生成式inpainting；新增的Gaussian anchor被局部优化而不修改已有表示。

Result: 在DIV2K上，ORCA在所报告的全部指标上优于VistaDream，MUSIQ由61.60提升至68.71，CLIP-IQA由0.474提升至0.574。

Conclusion: 结果表明，许多新视角伪影可仅靠复用重建场景中已有信息有效修复，无需依赖生成式补全；减少不必要的生成可抑制幻觉并更好保留原场景的内容与结构。

Abstract: Novel-view synthesis from a single image is a fundamentally ambiguous problem. As the camera moves away from the input viewpoint, previously hidden regions become visible, exposing missing geometry and holes in the reconstructed scene. Existing methods often rely on generative models to complete such regions. However, many of these artifacts are small gaps near depth boundaries and do not require generating new scene content. In order to eliminate expensive process of generating image we introduce ORCA, an occlusion-aware method for reconstructing and completing explorable 3D scenes from a single image. ORCA first introduces 3D structure into a Gaussian-anchor representation using monocular depth while preserving the original camera-ray correspondence. During scene exploration, missing regions are handled based on their size and structure. Small disocclusions are repaired using RGB-D information already available in the reconstruction, while generative inpainting is reserved for larger regions that cannot be reliably recovered from the scene. New Gaussian anchors are added and optimized locally without modifying the existing representation. By reducing unnecessary reliance on generative inpainting, ORCA limits generation-induced hallucinations and better preserves the content and structure of the original scene. On DIV2K, ORCA improves novel-view quality over VistaDream across all reported metrics, increasing MUSIQ from 61.60 to 68.71 and CLIP-IQA from 0.474 to 0.574. These results show that many novel-view artifacts can be repaired effectively by reusing information already present in the reconstructed scene.

</details>


### [92] [Tables Decoded: DELTA for Structure, TARQA for Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17458)
*Jahanvi Rajput,Dhruv Kudale,Saikiran Kasturi,Utkarsh Verma,Ganesh Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DELTA 表格理解框架：分离物理结构识别、逻辑结构识别与 OCR，用紧凑统一的 OTSL 语言表达表格；并在此基础上微调 LLM 得到 TARQA。在 FinTabNet/PubTabNet/PubTables-1M 上 TSR 达到 SOTA 水平，在 WTQ 与 FinTabNetQA 上分别提升 9.3 与 9.2 个百分点，并发布印地语基准 TORQUE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格重建与表格视觉问答（TabVQA）主要依赖在表格图像上运行的视觉语言模型（VLM），存在需要语言相关视觉编码器、处理成本高、对 LLM 不够友好、多语言文档适配差等问题。作者希望以结构化文本表示作为更可扩展、更有效的替代方案。

Method: 将表格理解拆分为物理结构识别、逻辑结构识别与 OCR 三个子模块，输出统一的 Optimised Table Structure Language（OTSL），以紧凑格式编码单元格排布与文本内容；在此基础上提出 TARQA，一个在 OTSL 序列上微调的 LLM，用于表格问答与表格视觉问答。

Result: 在 TSR 上，DELTA 在 FinTabNet、PubTabNet、PubTables-1M 上的 TEDS-Structure 与 SOTA 方法相当；在自建印地语基准 TORQUE 上验证非英语表格鲁棒性；TARQA 在 WTQ（TabQA）提升 9.3 个百分点，在 FinTabNetQA（TabVQA）提升 9.2 个百分点；在 TORQUE 上位列所有 VLM 与 DELTA+LLM 变体第二。

Conclusion: 结构化文本表格表示（OTSL）可替代表格图像输入，兼顾精度与可扩展性，避免语言相关视觉编码器，利于多语言文档场景；该路线在 TSR 与 TabQA/TabVQA 上均取得明显收益，并开源代码、模型与基准。

Abstract: Table understanding is a core task in document intelligence, encompassing two key subtasks: table reconstruction and table visual question answering (TabVQA). While recent approaches predominantly rely on vision- language models (VLMs) operating on table images, we propose a more scalable and effective alternative based on structured textual representations. These representations are easier to process, align more naturally with LLMs, and eliminate the need for language-specific visual encoders, making them particularly suitable for multilingual documents. We present DELTA, which separates physical structure recognition, logical structure recognition, and OCR to extract both layout and content accurately. DELTA outputs tables in Optimised Table Structure Language (OTSL), a compact and unified format that encodes cell arrangements and textual content. On table structure recognition (TSR), DELTA achieves TEDS- Structure scores comparable with state-of-the-art methods across FinTabNet, PubTabNet, and PubTables-1M. We further establish its robustness on non-English tables through our curated Hindi benchmark, TORQUE. Building on this, we introduce TARQA, an LLM fine-tuned on OTSL sequences. Our approach yields gains of 9.3 p.p. on WTQ (TabQA) and 9.2 p.p. on FinTabNetQA (TabVQA), respectively. On TORQUE, our method ranks second among all VLMs and DELTA + LLM variants. We release our code, models, and benchmark at: this https URL

</details>


### [93] [Det-LIME: Detector-Aware, Multi-Instance Local Interpretable Model-Agnostic Explanations for Automated Marine Mammal Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17479)
*Jiayi Zhou,David W. Johnston,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Det-LIME，一种面向目标检测器的多实例可解释性方法，通过逐检测加权、邻近核与 IoU 匹配生成实例级、框对齐的高分辨率解释，在港海豹航拍检测与海鸟案例上优于 vanilla LIME、Stabilized LIME、Deterministic LIME 及梯度归因方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑箱检测器在海洋哺乳动物研究中迅速普及，但可解释性技术很少融入保护工作流；现有面向分类的解释工具不适配检测任务，尤其对群居/集群生物影像会忽略场景中的多个目标，输出单实例、低分辨率且生物学意义有限的视觉解释，难以用于调试、定向数据增强与数据采集优化。

Method: Det-LIME：对 LIME 进行检测器感知的多实例改造。核心包括（1）逐检测加权，（2）强调每个检测框附近区域的邻近核，（3）基于 IoU 的匹配以在扰动间追踪同一实例，从而为每个实例生成框对齐的独立解释。评估：航拍无人机影像的港海豹检测为主动任务，海鸟为泛化性案例；对比 vanilla LIME、Stabilized LIME、Deterministic LIME 与梯度归因方法，指标为 Attribution Ratio 和 Max Saliency Hit Rate。

Result: Det-LIME 在多实例归因上持续优于所有对比方法，按 Attribution Ratio 与 Max Saliency Hit Rate 指标均有稳定提升；生成的分辨率更高、实例感知的解释可支撑后处理、调试以及建模与数据采集/增强的改进。

Conclusion: Det-LIME 为多实例检测场景提供了实例级、框对齐的可解释性方案，弥补分类型解释工具在检测任务中的不足；该思路可迁移到其他群居或集群生物检测场景，并服务于保护工作流中的调试、数据增强与采集决策。

Abstract: Despite the rapid uptake of black-box object detectors in marine mammal research and monitoring, explainability techniques are rarely integrated into conservation workflows. Furthermore, most classification-oriented explainability tools are ill-suited to detection tasks involving imagery of social organisms or those with colonial life histories, as they ignore multiple detections within a scene and produce single-instance outputs that blur evidence across individuals. These methods also generate low-resolution, often biologically irrelevant visuals, limiting their utility for debugging, targeted data augmentation, and refined data collection. We proposed Det-LIME, a detector-aware, multi-instance adaptation of Local Interpretable Model-Agnostic Explanations (LIME) that produced instance-specific, box-aligned explanations by combining per-detection weighting, a proximity kernel that emphasizes regions near each box, and Intersection-over-Union-based matching to track the same instance across perturbations. We evaluated Det-LIME on aerial drone imagery for harbor seal detection, with an additional seabird case study to assess generality, and compared it with vanilla LIME, Stabilized LIME, Deterministic LIME, and gradient-based attribution methods. Using the Attribution Ratio and Max Saliency Hit Rate metrics, we showed that Det-LIME consistently improved multi-instance attribution. In practice, these higher-resolution, instance-aware explanations provide insight into model outputs and support post-processing, debugging, and actionable improvements in modeling and data collection or augmentation.

</details>


### [94] [PhysStream: Streaming Physics-Grounded Video Generation with Structured Scene Memory and Fine-Grained Motion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17521)
*Chuhao Chen,Peter Wonka,Chaoyang Wang,Chen Wang,Qiao Feng,Sergey Tulyakov,Lingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhysStream，一个物理接地的自回归图像到视频生成模型：引入结构化场景记忆（位置图与物体跟踪图，在线源自先前生成帧），并支持稀疏速度增量信号作为细粒度运动控制，实现生成中途可交互控制的桌面刚体多物体场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控视频生成方法存在两大局限：一是需要生成前提供完整控制时序（无法生成中途交互控制），二是使用像素空间信号只规定物体位置而非物理动力学，缺乏物理一致性。作者希望以物理量（速度增量）驱动的稀疏信号，让模型学习底层动力学，实现细粒度、物理有意义的动态场景操控。

Method: 两阶段训练：先对双向模型进行运动控制条件微调；再训练因果自回归模型并加入结构化场景记忆（位置图+物体跟踪图，从已生成帧在线导出）。控制接口为稀疏的速度增量信号（编码物理量），结合自回归与在线场景记忆实现逐步生成与中途控制。

Result: 支持多物体桌面刚体场景的生成中途交互控制（先前方法不具备的能力）；在合成基准上运动分布距离（FVMD）降低33%、轨迹误差降低12%；真实野外对比中超过85%的人类评估者更偏好该模型。

Conclusion: PhysStream表明，将物理量化的稀疏控制信号与在线结构化场景记忆结合的自回归框架，可在图像到视频生成中实现细粒度、可交互、物理一致的动力学操控，并可推广至更复杂的物理场景与世界模型。

Abstract: Interactive control for video generation is moving from coarse prompts toward fine-grained, physically meaningful manipulation of dynamic scenes. Yet existing controllable methods either require the full control schedule before generation starts, or use pixel-space signals that dictate object positions rather than physical dynamics. To address these limitations, we propose PhysStream, an autoregressive model for physics-grounded image-to-video synthesis that incorporates structured scene memory---positional maps and object tracking maps derived online from previously generated frames---and supports fine-grained motion control via sparse velocity-increment signals that encode physical quantities, letting the model learn the underlying dynamics. We train our model in two stages: a bidirectional model is first finetuned with motion-control conditioning, then a causal autoregressive model is trained with additional structured scene memory, further improving physical consistency. PhysStream enables interactive, mid-generation control over multi-object tabletop rigid-body scenes---a capability not supported by prior methods---reducing motion distribution distance (FVMD) by 33% and trajectory error by 12% over the strongest baselines on synthetic benchmarks, and is preferred by human evaluators in over 85% of in-the-wild comparisons. Please check our website for more details: this https URL

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [Bi-MoDe: Bilateral Control-based Imitation Learning via Modifier-Conditioned Decoding for Modulation of Execution Speed and Contact Intensity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16040)
*Takumi Kobayashi,Masato Kobayashi,Yuki Uranishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Bi-MoDe：在Transformer动作解码器每层通过adaLN-Zero注入受约束潜变量，实现可指定"快慢/轻狠"行为修饰的模仿学习框架，并验证解码器条件化与潜空间组合的互补作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于双边控制（bilateral control）的模仿学习同时保留位置与力信息，适合接触丰富（contact-rich）操作；但已有方法在推理时缺乏让操作者指定任务执行风格（如缓慢/快速、轻柔/用力）的手段。

Method: 提出Bi-MoDe——修饰条件化解码框架：将受约束的潜变量（constrained latent）通过adaLN-Zero注入动作解码器（action decoder）的每一层，使行为指令直接影响action chunk生成；在真实白板擦除（whiteboard wiping）任务上，组合时间修饰与物理修饰进行评测，并做消融分析。

Result: Bi-MoDe相比action-chunking基线提升了物理指令遵循能力，同时保持时间控制（temporal control）的可比水平；消融显示解码器条件化与潜空间组合之间存在交互，两者结合对准确的物理指令遵循很重要。

Conclusion: 在接触丰富操作中，将修饰潜变量逐层注入Transformer解码器，可有效赋予模仿学习策略可调节的执行风格控制，且解码器条件化与潜空间组合的协同设计是关键。

Abstract: Bilateral control-based imitation learning captures both position and force information, making it well suited to contact-rich manipulation. However, existing approaches provide limited means for an operator to specify how a learned task should be executed at inference time, such as slowly or quickly, gently or firmly. We propose Bi-MoDe, a modifier-conditioned decoding framework that injects a constrained latent into every layer of the Transformer action decoder via adaLN-Zero, allowing behavioral directives to directly influence action-chunk generation. We evaluate the method on a real-world whiteboard wiping task with combinations of temporal and physical modifiers. Bi-MoDe improves physical directive following over the action-chunking baseline while maintaining comparable temporal control. An ablation further shows that decoder conditioning and latent-space composition interact, and that their combination is important for accurate physical directive following. Additional material is available at the this https URL

</details>


### [96] [MR-GLi: Mixed Reality-Based Gripper-Linked Overlays for Underwater Robot Arm Teleoperation via Bilateral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16041)
*Masashi Sasago,Masato Kobayashi,Yuki Uranishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MR-GLi 混合现实界面，将反作用力矩指示器与腕部相机图像空间配准到机器人夹爪上，用于水下双边遥操作，并以 2D 监视器为对照进行被试内实验。结果显示力反馈调节性能相当，但主观负担降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉力反馈（力矩反馈）可支持水下双边遥操作，但混合现实相对传统 2D 监视器呈现是否带来增益尚不明确，尤其缺乏对"信息获取方式"而非仅"任务表现"的评估。

Method: 设计 MR-GLi 界面：将反作用力矩指示器与腕部相机图像空间配准（spatial registration）到机器人夹爪，实现夹爪联动的视觉反馈获取。20 名被试在平衡化的被试内设计中完成抓取放置与举升任务，物体分刚性与柔性，视觉反馈内容与四通道双边控制保持一致，仅比较 MR 与 2D 监视器两种呈现方式。

Result: MR-GLi 在力矩调节性能上与 2D 监视器相当，未出现显著下降；主观评价显示在"从工作空间切换到视觉反馈"过程中感知负担降低。

Conclusion: 验证了夹爪联动式 MR 叠加层用于水下双边遥操作的可行性，并指出评估遥操作界面时除任务表现外还应考虑信息获取成本。

Abstract: Visual torque feedback supports underwater bilateral teleoperation, but the benefit of mixed reality (MR) over conventional monitor presentation remains unclear. We present MR-GLi, an MR interface that spatially registers a reaction torque indicator and wrist-camera image to the robot gripper. Twenty participants performed lift and pick-and-place tasks with rigid and compliant objects in a counterbalanced within-subject comparison with a 2D monitor, using identical visual-feedback content and four-channel bilateral control. MR-GLi provided gripper-linked access to visual feedback while maintaining a similar level of torque-regulation performance to the 2D monitor. Subjective evaluation further indicated reduced perceived burden associated with shifting attention between the workspace and visual feedback. These results demonstrate the feasibility of gripper-linked MR overlays for underwater bilateral teleoperation and highlight the importance of considering information access in addition to task performance. Additional material: this https URL

</details>


### [97] [World-Action Models for Robot Learning and Control: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16074)
*Zuxing Lu,Hongjia Zhai,Guanzhi Wang,Huajian Zeng,Jiaqi Yang,Jingyu Liu,Lei Cheng,Yuantai Zhang,Yuheng Qiu,Zezhou Cheng,Ivan Laptev,Danfei Xu,Benjamin Riviere,Giuseppe Loianno,Eric Xing,Xingxing Zuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文是一篇面向机器人学的World-Action Models（WAMs）综述，厘清其与传统世界模型、基于模型的强化学习、动作条件视频生成及反应式VLA策略的边界，并以统一分类法组织现有方法，覆盖表征、转移建模、动作接口、架构、训练流程、数据模态与规模化策略，同时梳理操作、导航、自动驾驶应用及数据集/基准/指标，最后讨论动作对齐、世界-动作解耦、空间与多视图一致性、长时记忆、闭环神经验仿真与高效推理等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放环境中的机器人受部分可观测性、物理约束与动态任务情境影响，仅将观测和语言指令映射为动作不足以保证可靠决策；机器人需预测候选动作对未来的影响。世界模型、视频生成与VLA策略的进展催生了将未来世界预测与可执行动作生成耦合的World-Action Models，然而该方向术语混乱、边界不清、方法分散，亟需一篇面向机器人学的系统性综述以整合预测式世界建模与动作生成。

Method: 综述性方法：界定WAM范围并与常规世界模型、model-based RL、action-conditioned视频生成、反应式VLA策略对比；提出统一分类维度（表征、transition modeling、action interface、架构、训练pipeline、数据模态、scaling策略）；按操作、导航、自动驾驶分应用梳理；汇总数据集、benchmark、metrics与评测协议；归纳挑战。

Result: 无原始实验结果。综述性产出包括：WAM的概念界定与与相邻范式的对比、覆盖多维度的方法分类体系、跨操作/导航/自动驾驶的应用综述、评测资源（数据集、基准、指标、协议）汇总，以及对动作对齐、world-action factorization、空间与多视图一致性、长时记忆、neural simulation用于闭环策略学习、高效推理等挑战的梳理。

Conclusion: 该综述为将预测式世界建模与动作生成相结合提供简洁的技术基础，指向更可靠的具身机器人智能；作为领域入门与术语/分类对齐的参考框架有价值，但不提供新方法或实验证据。

Abstract: Robots operating in open environments act under partial observability, physical constraints, and dynamic task contexts. Beyond mapping observations and language instructions to actions, they must anticipate how candidate actions may affect future states and task-relevant outcomes. Recent advances in world models, video generation, and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies have motivated the development of World-Action Models (WAMs), which couple future world prediction with executable action generation. This survey provides a robotics-oriented review of WAMs. We clarify their scope relative to conventional world models, model-based reinforcement learning, action-conditioned video generation, and reactive VLA policies, and organize existing methods through a unified taxonomy covering representations, transition modeling, action interfaces, architectures, training pipelines, data modalities, and scaling strategies. We further review applications of WAMs in manipulation, navigation, and autonomous driving, and we summarize the datasets, benchmarks, metrics, and protocols used to evaluate WAM systems. Finally, we discuss key challenges in action alignment, world-action factorization, spatial and multi-view consistency, long-horizon memory, neural simulation for closed-loop policy learning, and efficient inference. Taken together, this survey aims to provide a concise technical foundation for integrating predictive world modeling with action generation, toward more reliable embodied robot intelligence. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [98] [AssemblyGrid v1: A Benchmark for Multi-Robot Production with Temporary Coalitions, Local Information, and Geometric Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16075)
*Fouad Bahrpeyma,David Heik,Dirk Reichelt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 AssemblyGrid v1，一个面向柔性多机器人生产、可复现的任务级基准，统一建模工序推进、物料流转、临时联盟与并发执行，并在集中式与分布式控制及 MARL 方法上完成验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性机器人生产需要工序、路由、资源分配、临时协作与并发执行的联合决策，各决策互相影响可行性；去中心化控制下每个机器人只有局部信息，系统进展依赖集体决策、共享资源与工位兼容性，现有基准缺乏将工序推进、部分可观测与资源竞争统一到同一任务级形式化的可复现平台。

Method: 构建 AssemblyGrid v1 基准：任务级形式化中显式包含工序推进、去中心化观测、物料转移、临时多机器人联盟、生产性并发与几何相关可行性；设计 Flow、Coalition、Concurrency 三个工作负载族，每族含三个场景级别；任务成功与评价指标独立于学习奖励与求解方法；通过可执行一致性检查、机制研究与算法实验进行评估，对比特权集中式参考、结构化去中心化控制器及 IPPO、MAPPO、QMIX 等 MARL 方法。

Result: 可执行一致性检查通过，机制研究确认各要素发挥作用；集中式与去中心化控制下均实现有效生产执行；MARL 实验表明去中心化策略仅凭局部观测与动作即可学到有效生产行为。

Conclusion: AssemblyGrid v1 为研究柔性机器人生产中的协作决策提供了可控、可复现、方法无关的基准，支持学习与非学习方法在同一生产问题上进行公平比较。

Abstract: Flexible robotic production requires joint decisions on process progression, material routing, resource assignment, temporary cooperation, and simultaneous execution, since each decision can affect the feasibility of the others. The challenge is greater under decentralized control, where each robot acts from bounded local information while system progress depends on collective decisions, shared resources, material state, and workspace compatibility. These properties closely match cooperative multi-agent decision making under partial observability and resource contention. This paper introduces AssemblyGrid v1, a reproducible benchmark for repeated multi-robot production that combines explicit process progression, decentralized observations, material transfer, temporary multi-robot coalitions, productive concurrency, and geometry-dependent feasibility within one task-level formulation. The benchmark includes Flow, Coalition, and Concurrency workload families, each with three scenario levels. Task success and evaluation measures are defined independently of learning reward and solution method, allowing learning-based and non-learning methods to address the same production problem. AssemblyGrid v1 is evaluated through executable conformance checks, mechanism studies, and algorithmic experiments using a privileged centralized reference, structured decentralized controllers, and MARL methods including IPPO, MAPPO, and QMIX. Results demonstrate productive execution under centralized and decentralized control. The MARL experiments further show that decentralized policies can learn effective production behavior from local observations and actions, supporting AssemblyGrid as a controlled benchmark for studying cooperative decision making in flexible robotic production.

</details>


### [99] [Structure-Preserving Quantum Circuit Architectures for Robot Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16089)
*Andrea Morghen,Pierluigi Arpenti,Roberto Schiattarella,Giovanni Acampora,Bruno Siciliano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种量子表示与电路架构，用于刚体变换，并专门化为串联开链机械臂的Denavit–Hartenberg运动学，通过单量子比特Bloch矢量编码单位方向、选择器寄存器编码幅值概率，由读出量子比特的约化态实现归一化加权求和，从而重建末端位置与姿态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化空间数据需要既能保持几何关系、又可暴露可测量观测量、并且能在有限深度硬件上实现的量子编码；当前缺乏针对刚体变换与机械臂运动学的几何可解释量子表示。

Method: 将平移贡献分解为经典度量幅值与单量子比特Bloch矢量编码的带符号单位方向；参数化旋转复现坐标系的顺序传播；用选择器寄存器制备与幅值成正比的概率分布；以指定读出量子比特的约化态编码归一化加权和，再结合保留的经典尺度重建末端执行器位置；另用两个读出量子比特编码末端坐标系轴向。并给出多种替代电路架构，在量子比特数、电路深度、受控操作与测量需求上取舍。

Result: 在理想期望值层面，测量的Pauli观测量可复现对应经典运动学量；串联机械臂验证中，理想模拟下位置与姿态重建误差数值上可忽略；有限采样模拟、含噪执行、转译分析与硬件演示进一步刻画了统计误差、噪声敏感性与实现开销。

Conclusion: 该量子表示提供了紧凑且几何可解释的姿态接口，并在理想条件下复现经典运动学量；但作者明确指出未主张任何计算优势，其价值在于表示与硬件实现的可行性刻画。

Abstract: Structured spatial data require quantum encodings that preserve geometric relations, expose measurable observables, and remain implementable on finite-depth hardware. This work introduces a quantum representation and circuit architecture for rigid-body transformations and specializes it to Denavit--Hartenberg kinematics of serial open-chain manipulators. Each translational contribution is factorized into a classical metric magnitude and a signed unit direction encoded by a single-qubit Bloch vector, while parameterized rotations reproduce the ordered propagation of frame directions. A selector register prepares probabilities proportional to the contribution magnitudes, and the reduced state of a designated readout qubit encodes their normalized weighted sum. The retained classical scale then reconstructs the metric end-effector position. Two additional readout qubits encode terminal-frame axes, providing a compact and geometrically interpretable pose interface. At the ideal expectation-value level, measured Pauli observables reproduce the corresponding classical kinematic quantities. Alternative circuit architectures realize the same representation with different tradeoffs in qubit count, circuit depth, controlled operations, and measurement requirements. Validation on a serial manipulator yields numerically negligible position and orientation reconstruction errors under ideal simulation. Finite-shot simulations, noisy executions, transpilation analysis, and a hardware demonstration further characterize statistical error, noise sensitivity, and implementation overhead without asserting computational advantage.

</details>


### [100] [Occupancy Network-Guided Autonomous Robotic Partial Nephrectomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16186)
*Ethan Kilmer,Pit Henrich,Jiawei Ge,Paul M. Scheikl,Laura Connolly,Soum D. Lokeshwar,Joseph Chen,Justin D. Opfermann,Kaitlyn Kumar,Lauren Shepard,Ahmed Ghazi,Nirmish Singla,Richard J. Cha,Kevin Cleary,Franziska Mathis-Ullrich,Axel Krieger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首个视觉引导的自主软组织肿瘤切除系统：用物理仿真训练的条件占用网络，从单视图部分点云推断完整3D解剖结构，实现部分肾切除的闭环自适应规划与执行，在患者来源水凝胶体模上完成8例连续自主切除（77次电外科切割），全部达到阴性切缘，平均绝对切缘误差1.61±0.48 mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主软组织癌症手术长期局限于器官表面操作，因为现有系统在组织变形或被切割后无法感知与适应解剖结构的变化，缺乏对软组织三维形变与切除的动态建模能力，因此无法完成实体肿瘤的完整切除。

Method: 系统由三部分构成：(1) 条件占用网络，完全在基于物理的仿真中训练，从单视图部分点云推断肿瘤、边缘组织与肾脏的完整3D解剖，并在组织被切割/变形时维持术中跟踪；(2) 硬件平台：深度相机采集表面点云、双臂机器人分别执行电外科切割与真空组织牵拉；(3) 面向肿瘤切除的自主控制策略，实现自适应规划与执行，构成闭环影像驱动流程。

Result: 在患者来源水凝胶体模的开放部分肾切除场景中，机器人连续完成8例自主肿瘤切除，包含77次电外科切割，所有切割均实现阴性手术切缘，平均绝对切缘误差为1.61±0.48 mm。

Conclusion: 该工作首次展示了监督式自主、闭环、影像驱动、切缘阴性肿瘤切除的基础方案，证明仿真训练的占用网络可支撑软组织形变下的实时感知与自适应操作；当前验证限于体模，尚需向活体组织与临床场景推进。

Abstract: Autonomous soft-tissue cancer surgery has been limited to interventions on organ surfaces, because current systems cannot perceive and adapt to anatomy once it deforms or is cut. We introduce the first vision-guided autonomous system capable of performing complete tumor resections for partial nephrectomy. Our system integrates conditional occupancy networks, trained entirely in a physics-based simulation, that infer full 3-D anatomy (tumor, margin tissue, and kidney) from single-view partial point clouds. These occupancy networks maintain intraoperative tracking even as tissue is cut and deformed, enabling adaptive planning and execution. The surgical platform combines a depth camera for capturing surface point clouds, dual robotic arms for electrosurgical cutting and vacuum-based tissue manipulation, and an autonomous control strategy for tumor resection. In patient-derived hydrogel phantoms under an open partial nephrectomy setting, the robot performed eight consecutive autonomous tumor resections comprising 77 electrosurgical cuts, with all cuts achieving negative surgical margins and 1.61 $\pm$ 0.48 mm mean absolute margin error. This work demonstrates, for the first time, a foundation for supervised autonomous closed-loop, imaging-driven, margin-negative tumor removal in phantoms.

</details>


### [101] [Tendon-Driven Continuum Robot with Modular Stiffness and In-Situ Self Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16256)
*Guo Ning,Zheng Cao,Junzhe Hu,Xiangyun Bu,David Quinn,Tiancheng Wu,Zackory Erickson,Carmel Majidi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种模块化自包含连续体机器人平台，结合可更换关节（刚度解析预计算）与基于磁传感器+模块化学习的本体感觉估计，单一关节模型可跨构型复用，实现无外部跟踪的自我感知与形态自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续体机器人多为任务专用且依赖外部传感基础设施（如外部视觉跟踪），缺乏可重构性与自主感知能力，难以快速组装和实际部署。

Method: 1）机械层面：可互换连续体关节，刚度经解析预计算，支持快速组装与形状直接编程；2）感知层面：磁传感器获取本体信号，配合模块化学习框架，按关节训练单一模型并在不同构型间复用，从而推断形态/位姿。

Result: 在真实环境中完成实验验证，展示了自感知能力与构型自适应，无需任何外部跟踪设备。摘要未给出定量精度或对比基线。

Conclusion: 自包含+模块化+可重构是一条可行的连续体机器人路径，能以极低标定成本实现本体感觉与形态自适应，为真实场景部署提供基础平台。但缺少定量精度、泛化范围与开源信息。

Abstract: Continuum robots enable smooth shape morphing and safe interaction in confined environments. However, most existing systems are task-specific and depend on external sensing infrastructure, limiting their adaptability and real-world deployment. This paper presents a self-contained modular continuum robotic platform that combines mechanical reconfigurability with onboard pose estimation. The robot is constructed from interchangeable continuum joints with analytically precomputed stiffness, allowing rapid assembly and direct programming of the robot shape. Proprioceptive sensing is achieved using magnetic sensors and a modular learning-based framework, where a single model is trained per joint and reused across configurations. The system is experimentally validated in real world, demonstrating self-sensing capabilities and adaptation without external tracking.

</details>


### [102] [ConGraspXL: Controllable Constraint-Conditioned Dexterous Grasping Motion Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16319)
*Hui Zhang,Mirko Meboldt,Jie Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ConGraspXL 在 GraspXL 基础上引入分层约束建模与掩码残差接口，实现多任务驱动约束（接近方向、接触区域、腕部轨迹、功能手势）及其组合的可控抓取动作合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧抓取动作合成（如作者前作 GraspXL）虽具备对多种物体与手部形态的可扩展泛化能力，但缺乏对任务驱动约束（指定接近方向、期望接触区域、腕部轨迹、功能性手姿等异质约束）的可控性，难以支撑下游整体身体抓取补全、功能性抓取等应用。

Method: 提出 ConGraspXL：1）分层约束表示（hierarchical constraint formulation），将异质约束组织为可统一处理的层级结构；2）掩码残差接口（masked residual interface）实现约束的灵活组合；3）动态手部中心（dynamic hand centers）与前馈腕部引导（feed-forward wrist guidance）提升控制精度。整体作为即插即用的低层抓取控制器，保留 GraspXL 的泛化性。

Result: 摘要为定性描述：在保持 GraspXL 强泛化能力的同时，对各类单一约束及其组合均可实现精准、灵活的可控性，并成功支持整体身体抓取补全、功能性抓取与人体动作模仿等下游应用。

Conclusion: ConGraspXL 将可扩展抓取动作合成扩展为约束条件下的可控合成，可作为下游任务即插即用的低层抓取控制器，为任务导向灵巧操作提供可控基础模块。

Abstract: Dexterous grasping is usually conducted for specific tasks, leading to heterogeneous constraints such as specific approach directions, desired contact regions, specified wrist trajectories, and functional hand poses. Our previous work, GraspXL, achieves scalable grasping motion synthesis for diverse objects and hand morphologies, while lacking controllability for synthesis under such various task-driven constraints. In this paper, we propose ConGraspXL, which extends GraspXL with controllable constraint-conditioned grasp motion synthesis that accommodates diverse task-driven constraints and their combinations. We introduce a hierarchical constraint formulation, enable flexible constraint composition with a masked residual interface, and improve control precision with dynamic hand centers and feed-forward wrist guidance. Without losing the strong generalization capabilities of GraspXL, ConGraspXL enables precise and flexible controllability for various individual constraints and their combinations, providing a plug-and-play low-level grasp controller for downstream applications such as whole-body grasp completion, functional grasping, and human-motion imitation.

</details>


### [103] [ManiSkillFormer: Demonstration-Free Compositional Manipulation via Task-Conditioned Geometric Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16331)
*Peiqi Yu,Mosam Dabhi,Shangtao Li,Bowei Li,Laszlo Jeni,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ManiSkillFormer: neuro-symbolic framework using task-conditioned geometric contracts (keypoints, surface normals) generated by LLM agents to enable demonstration-free, compositional robotic manipulation with reusable motion templates.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端视动策略难以复用与组合，且依赖每物体微调或额外示范；作者希望通过显式的几何契约结构化解耦感知与动作，实现免示范、可组合的操作。

Method: 神经符号框架：每个操作技能声明所需几何原语（关键点、表面法向）；由LLM agent生成任务条件化的几何契约与运动模板；契约引导感知模块从观测中grounding任务相关3D原语，再实例化技能库中的可复用运动模板。

Result: 在Galaxea R1-Lite双臂机器人上三设定评估：8类30实例零样本抓放88.24%成功率；功能操作（拧、倒、按、折）平均75.00%；3个长时域任务完成率50–80%，均优于基线及两条消融流程。

Conclusion: 该设计无需逐物体策略微调或额外机器人示范，即可跨物体与任务实现可组合、可复用的操作。

Abstract: We present ManiSkillFormer, a neuro-symbolic framework for demonstration-free and compositional robotic manipulation. Instead of learning end-to-end visuomotor policies, ManiSkillFormer introduces task-conditioned geometric contracts that explicitly structure the interface between perception and action. Each manipulation skill declares the semantic geometric primitives required for execution, such as object keypoints and surface normals. Building on human-defined skill structures, LLM agents generate these contracts and corresponding motion templates for different objects and task contexts. These contracts guide the perception module to ground task-relevant 3D primitives from observations, which are then used to instantiate reusable motion templates stored in a skill library. We evaluate ManiSkillFormer on Galaxea R1-Lite dual-arm robot across three settings: zero-shot pick-and-place over 8 object categories with 30 different instances, functional manipulation tasks including unscrewing, pouring, pressing, and folding, and 3 long-horizon tasks. ManiSkillFormer achieves higher average success rates than the evaluated baselines and two ablated pipelines: 88.24% for demonstration-free pick-and-place, 75.00% average success on functional manipulation and 50--80% completion rates across the long-horizon tasks. These results show that our design enables composable and reusable manipulation across objects and tasks without per-object policy fine-tuning or additional robot demonstrations.

</details>


### [104] [Auto-HSI: Personalized human control of a robot swarm on demand by using LLMs for online automatic code generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16346)
*Alessandro Nazzari,Nathan Cerisara,Dorian Tonnis,Raina Zakir,Lorenzo Labarile,Weixu Zhu,Marco Dorigo,Mary Katherine Heinrich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Auto-HSI 通过自然语言描述与手势示教，自动生成个性化的人-集群交互状态机代码，并在 50 台仿真机器人及真机上验证了集中式遥操作（运动、队形、形变）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未经训练的操作者难以编程控制机器人集群；现有 HSI 接口通常固定、不可定制，需要专家介入。作者希望让操作者用自然语言+手势描述期望的集群行为，由系统自动生成个性化控制接口代码。

Method: Auto-HSI 原型：输入为自然语言描述与手势示教，输出为个性化有限状态机代码，用于集中式控制。支持单手/双手手势，可遥操作机器人运动、队形形状与形状形变。对gesture tracking与code generation模块分别做性能基准测试。

Result: 在物理仿真器中由真人操作者集中控制 50 台模拟机器人，在正常与噪声条件下完成任务：射门得分、穿越需形变的迷宫、分成两组同时射入两球；并演示了操作中实时更新个性化接口，以及真机运行。

Conclusion: Auto-HSI 证明按需生成个性化 HSI 接口可行，将自然语言与手势示教转化为可执行的集群控制代码，并在仿真与真机上完成端到端验证。

Abstract: This paper presents Auto-HSI, a method for generating personalized human-swarm interaction (HSI) interfaces on demand. The objective is to enable untrained operators to use natural language descriptions and gesture demonstrations to explain how they want the robots to collectively behave in response to their gestures. Based on these inputs, the code should automatically be generated for personalized state machines that will control the robots as desired, in response to the desired gesture inputs. In the developed Auto-HSI prototype, the generated code produces a personalized interface for centralized control using one- and two-handed gestures, enabling a user to teleoperate the robots' motion, formation shape, and shape deformation. We test the gesture tracking and code generation components of Auto-HSI against performance benchmarks. We then test the full Auto-HSI prototype in ``live'' operation experiments, in which real human operators centrally control 50 simulated robots in a physics-based simulator, under nominal and noisy conditions. In these experiments, robots are teleoperated to: score a goal, traverse a maze that requires shape deformation, and score two simultaneous goals by splitting into two groups. We also demonstrate a real human operator making live updates to their personalized Auto-HSI interface during operation (in simulation). Finally, we demonstrate live operation of real robots.

</details>


### [105] [UDAV: Uncertainty-Driven Adaptive VLM Waypoint Planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16368)
*Ghazal Farhani,Shabnam Shabani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UDAV：用VLM随机采样轨迹的medoid作为标称路径、以空间离散度作为不确定性信号，超阈值时触发重评估，用于UAV引导的UGV越野导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）可直接从航拍影像生成越野导航路径，但预测不提供可靠性指示，导致大误差无法被识别或缓解。需要一种能同时给出更强标称路径与可操作不确定性信号的规划器。

Method: UDAV 对VLM进行多次随机轨迹采样，取中位轨迹（medoid）作为自洽标称路线，并以采样轨迹的空间离散度估计预测不确定性。当内部路点的最大不确定性超过阈值时，触发"重考虑"（reconsideration）阶段，否则直接返回medoid。在两次UAV飞行的400条留出轨迹查询上评估。

Result: 随机medoid选择将平均位移误差（ADE）从确定性预测的147.4像素降至115.9像素；完整规划器平均ADE为110.4像素，相对确定性规划下降25.1%，且所有查询均产生有效轨迹。UDAV在所有配置中取得最低的90/95分位误差，优于更高预算的K=10一致性基线；相对K=5 medoid，将90/95分位误差从225.3/326.0像素降至199.0/290.8像素。

Conclusion: 随机VLM预测既能提供更强的标称路径，也能给出可操作的不确定性信号，用以选择性缓解大规划误差，为越野UAV-UGV协同导航提供实用方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can generate routes directly from aerial imagery for off-road navigation, but their predictions provide no indication of reliability. We present UDAV, an Uncertainty-Driven Adaptive VLM Waypoint Planner for UAV-guided UGV navigation.
