Our frontend pairs consecutive range grids with a bilateral gated difference, while IMU-guided cross-attention dynamically routes directional features conditioned on platform kinematics. A Streaming Causal Transformer couples an uncompressed Local KV cache with compressed Chunk-FIFO memory, maintaining bounded inference cost and memory footprint independent of flight duration. In real-flight evaluations, TIO-FORMER reduces open-loop position error by 54.4% compared to nano-UAV optical flow and by 66.4%-89.1% over learned inertial baselines. We also evaluate performance across multiple environments and robustness under severe sensing degradation. Deployed on an edge RISC-V companion computer, TIO-FORMER achieves a P95 latency of 10.466 ms and peak resident memory of 6.324 MiB (less than 5 percent system RAM), demonstrating that sparse range sensing provides practical geometric anchoring for resource-constrained micro-aerial robots. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [142] [FluxVLA Engine: A One-Stop VLA Engineering Platform for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17210)
*Yinhao Li,Weixin Mao,Zihan Lan,Jikun Rong,Qirui Hu,Yiming Zhang,Weipeng Deng,Bowen Shen,Minzhao Zhu,Yiming Mao,Yan Yang,Chenguang Cui,Hongyuan Chen,Xu Huang,Zheyi Zhao,Pinxi Shen,Bozhen He,Zhen Fu,Yifan Wang,Zexin Zhang,Ang Gao,Haoyu Chen,Chengqi Shi,Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FluxVLA Engine 是一个开放、配置驱动的平台，将 VLA、WAM 与离线强化学习的异构组件统一为可复现的“数据到部署”流程，通过标准化接口、组合式双臂仿真、自动数据生成与人在回路校正，并集成 RTC 与加速推理后端实现实机执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA、世界动作模型与离线 RL 的设计空间迅速扩张，但数据格式、训练栈、评测协议、推理运行时与本体接口高度碎片化，导致有潜力的具身学习算法难以被可靠地复现与部署。论文的目标是解决这一工程瓶颈，而非再提出一个新的策略模型。

Method: 采用配置驱动的标准化接口设计，统一数据集、视觉语言与世界模型、动作头、奖励/优势加权学习、分布式训练、仿真评测、优化推理与机器人算子；集成组合式双臂仿真、可扩展自动数据生成、模型解耦的人在回路 rollout/接管/纠错采集/奖励标注；实时执行侧结合 Real-Time Chunking (RTC)、加速推理后端、轻量远程 GPU 服务与可配置轨迹后处理；以共享且可审计的契约连接离线学习、仿真验证、在线纠错与实机执行。

Result: 摘要为平台性工作介绍，未给出定量实验结论；其“结果”体现为形成一条覆盖离线学习—仿真验证—在线纠错—实机执行的统一且可审计工作流，宣称代码已开源。

Conclusion: FluxVLA 的贡献在于工程与系统层面：以标准化接口和可复现工作流打通具身学习的算法与部署链路，降低从算法到可靠机器人系统的集成成本，定位为面向复现评测与可信部署的基础设施。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models, world-action models (WAMs), and offline reinforcement learning methods are rapidly expanding the design space of embodied policies, yet turning these algorithms into reliable robot systems remains constrained by fragmented data formats, training stacks, evaluation protocols, inference runtimes, and embodiment-specific interfaces. We present $\mathrm{FluxVLA}$ Engine, an open, configuration-driven platform that turns heterogeneous embodied-policy components into a reproducible data-to-deployment workflow. Rather than introducing another policy model, $\mathrm{FluxVLA}$ standardizes interfaces for datasets, visual-language and world models, action heads, reward- or advantage-weighted learning, distributed training, simulation evaluation, optimized inference, and robot operators. The engine further integrates compositional dual-arm simulation, scalable automatic data generation, and model-decoupled human-in-the-loop rollout, takeover, correction collection, and reward annotation. For responsive physical execution, it combines Real-Time Chunking (RTC) with accelerated inference backends, lightweight remote GPU serving, and configurable trajectory post-processing. Together, these capabilities connect offline learning, simulation validation, online correction, and real-robot execution through shared and auditable contracts. $\mathrm{FluxVLA}$ therefore targets the engineering bottlenecks separating promising embodied-learning algorithms from reproducible evaluation and dependable deployment. Code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [143] [Swim-and-Breach at Palm Scale: A Rudder-Steered Two-Propeller Underwater Robot Platform with Differential-Thrust Pitch Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17240)
*Daehyun Choi,Ian Bergerson,Hengjia Zhu,Tianjun Lan,Saad Bhamla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一款掌心大小（65 mm、34 g）的水陆两栖（游动+跃出）机器人平台，通过双桨差速推力实现俯仰控制（PID闭环）、尾舵实现偏航控制，可完成巡航-跃出-巡航无缝序列并跨越干湿间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型水下机器人通常受限于体积与推力，难以在游动中主动控制俯仰姿态，因而无法完成从水中跃出、跨越干区间隙这类动作；而溪流、淹没结构、工业系统的巡检需要能穿越障碍与干湿断层的微型平台。

Method: 平台为65 mm、34 g的掌心尺度设计；两个垂直堆叠的螺旋桨同时提供推进力与差速俯仰控制力矩，俯仰由PID闭环控制，尾部舵面提供偏航控制；通过流场仿真评估并优化船体外形，螺旋桨采用B-series建模优化并经测力计（dynamometer）测量验证。

Result: 相比等效长方体，优化后船体阻力降低约5倍；游速达13.9倍体长/秒，转向角速度209°/秒，属水下机器人已报道的较高水平；自由游动中俯仰环可将机体调至任意指令抬头角，配合尾舵稳定出水姿态，实现1.6倍体长高度、3.7倍体长距离的跃出，并完成无缝的巡航-跃出-巡航序列。

Conclusion: 该平台可作为构建小型机器人的基础，用于跨越水池间障碍与干燥间隙，面向溪流、淹没结构及工业系统的巡检作业。工作重心在机械/流体设计与姿态控制，未涉及学习型策略、感知建模或开源发布。

Abstract: We present a palm-scale (65 mm, 34 g) swim-and-breach robot platform. Two vertically stacked propellers provide both propulsion and differential-thrust pitch control under a proportional-integral-derivative (PID) loop, and a tail rudder adds yaw control. The hull, evaluated by flow simulation, reduces the drag five-fold relative to an equivalent cuboid, and the propellers are optimized using B-series modeling validated by dynamometer measurements. The current robot swims at 13.9 body lengths per second and turns at 209 deg per second, corresponding to the upper limits reported for underwater robots. In free swimming, the pitch loop turns the body to any commanded nose-up pitch angle, and, with the rudder stabilizing the exit, the current robot leaps 1.6 body lengths high and 3.7 long in a seamless cruise-leap-cruise sequence. The platform can be used to build small-scale robots that cross barriers and dry gaps between pools for inspection in streams, flooded structures, and industrial systems.

</details>


### [144] [DriveMCP: An Agentic AI framework for Advanced Driver Assistance System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17247)
*Farzad Nadiri,Mehdi Cina,Ahmad B.Rad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DriveMCP将感知、法规合规推理、车辆状态解读与安全仲裁集成为模块化可审计的智能体驾驶辅助流水线，在CARLA中验证其可降低交通违规与超速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端VLM驾驶助手缺乏合规推理、车辆状态感知与安全仲裁，且限速/辖区等关键世界状态常由仿真器直接注入，脱离真实可获得性。

Method: DriveLM生成图结构场景理解与语言化驾驶信息；结构化解析层从VLM输出提取世界状态合规要素；有状态编排层协调MCP服务器（法规RAG、天气/牵引风险、CAN/OBD遥测）；融合产出结构化决策，再由RSS式护栏在有限在线自适应下仲裁"说话/行动"。

Result: 在多语言、跨境与动态限速的CARLA场景中，相较VLM-Direct、VLM-Direct+RAG、VLM-Tools-NoArbiter基线，降低交通违规与超速，改善危险响应时间，并保持亚秒级建议延迟。

Conclusion: 模块化MCP+合规RAG+RSS护栏路线可提升驾驶辅助的合规性与安全性，但验证局限于CARLA仿真，缺乏真实道路或实车部署证据。

Abstract: An agentic AI driver-assistance framework that integrates perception, compliance reasoning, vehicle-state interpretation, and safety arbitration into a modular and auditable pipeline. The architecture, referred to as DriveMCP, incorporates a sensor-like perception stack alongside DriveLM as the vision-language front end to generate a graph-structured scene understanding (Graph Visual Question Answering) and language-grounded driving information. Key compliance elements in world_state, including posted speed limits and jurisdiction cues, are derived from DriveLM outputs through a structured parsing layer rather than being injected as simulator ground truth. A stateful orchestration layer coordinates specialized experts exposed as Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers: (i) a Rules server that performs retrieval-augmented compliance reasoning over jurisdiction-specific traffic codes and sign conventions, (ii) a Weather server that estimates traction risk and contextual speed advisories, and (iii) an MCP-CAN server that surfaces Controller Area Network (CAN)/On-Board Diagnostics (OBD) telemetry and diagnostic context for health-aware risk shaping. These outputs are fused to generate a structured decision that prompts a recommended course of action. The outcome is then further filtered by a Responsibility-Sensitive Safety (RSS)-inspired guardrail that arbitrates speak versus act decisions under bounded online adaptation. In CARLA simulation across multilingual, cross-border, and dynamic speed-limit scenarios, DriveMCP reduces traffic infractions and overspeed relative to the VLM-Direct, VLM-Direct+RAG, and VLM-Tools-NoArbiter baselines, while improving hazard response time and maintaining sub-second advisory latency.

</details>


### [145] [Port-Hamiltonian Koopman Operator Synthesis for Mechanical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17249)
*Rajpal Singh,Aditya Singh,Jishnu Keshavan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于广义动量坐标的结构保持Koopman框架，将Euler-Lagrange系统的机械驱动表示为已知的、状态无关端口，通过结构约束神经网络联合学习提升函数与port-Hamiltonian Koopman生成元，使学习动力学"构造性被动"，并用Cayley中点离散精确保持离散时间存储-耗散平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯轨迹数据学习到的有限维Koopman模型容易违反底层力学的能量结构，导致预测出现人为能量增长，在递归传播下发散。作者希望在不依赖惩罚项或事后投影的前提下，从构造上保证学习动力学的被动性与稳定性。

Method: 1) 采用广义动量坐标的动量变换，将机械驱动暴露为已知的状态无关端口，并在提升动力学中显式保留；2) 设计结构约束神经网络联合学习提升函数与port-Hamiltonian Koopman生成元，使学得的动力学由构造即被动；3) 使用Cayley中点离散精确保持对应的存储-耗散平衡；4) 解析推导离散存储平衡方程及学习预测器的稳定性保证。

Result: 仿真与实验研究显示，相比Koopman基线，预测精度、数据效率和闭环跟踪性能均有提升，且系统维度越高增益越明显。

Conclusion: 将Koopman提升与port-Hamiltonian结构约束结合，可在Euler-Lagrange机器人系统上实现构造性被动、数值保结构的学习预测器，兼具理论稳定性保证与实证的性能、数据效率优势，为非线性机器人系统的高效线性预测与控制提供新路径。

Abstract: Finite-dimensional Koopman models enable efficient linear prediction and control of nonlinear robotic systems. However, models learned purely from trajectory data may violate the energetic structure of the underlying mechanics, producing predictions that exhibit artificial energy growth and diverge under recursive propagation. This work presents a structure-preserving Koopman framework for Euler-Lagrange systems built on generalized-momentum coordinates. The momentum transformation exposes the mechanical actuation as a known, state-independent port, which is preserved explicitly in the lifted dynamics. A structure-constrained neural architecture is developed to jointly learn the lifting functions and a port-Hamiltonian Koopman generator, rendering the learned dynamics passive by construction rather than through penalty terms or post-hoc projection. A Cayley-midpoint discretization further preserves the corresponding storage-dissipation balance exactly in discrete time. These properties are established analytically by deriving the discrete storage balance and associated stability guarantees of the learned predictor. Simulation and experimental studies demonstrate improved prediction accuracy, data efficiency, and closed-loop tracking over Koopman baselines, with increasing gains for higher-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [146] [CAD-Based Relation Learning and Geometric-Symbolic Planning for Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17263)
*Fabian Harlacher,Christian Friedrich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出混合装配序列规划框架：用神经网络从点云预测语义几何关系，经人在回路校正后转为符号装配图，再由几何-符号规划器结合可见性射线投射搜索可行的机器人拆解方向与操作原语，在ASAP数据集上达85.83%规划成功率，30+零件装配的规划中位时间较基线降低50倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 装配序列规划（ASP）具有组合爆炸特性，穷举规划对复杂工业装配不可行；同时大量CAD模型缺少可靠的语义接触信息或需大量人工预处理，限制了现有方法的适用性。作者希望在不完美CAD数据上实现高效、可扩展的机器人拆装序列规划。

Method: 1) 神经网络从点云预测语义几何关系；2) 人在回路验证，纠正不确定预测与规划失败；3) 将关系转换为符号装配图；4) 几何-符号规划器计算局部有效的机器人操作原语集合；5) 基于可见性的射线投射策略引导可行拆解方向搜索，避免穷举组合搜索，并利用局部解空间做序列优化。

Result: 在自建装配数据集与ASAP测试集上评估。ASAP上规划成功率达85.83%；在所有装配规模下中位规划时间降低一个数量级以上，对于超过30个零件的装配，相比基线降低超过50倍。

Conclusion: 学习式特征分割、人在回路验证与几何-符号推理相结合的混合框架，可在不完美CAD数据上高效完成机器人装配序列规划并大幅降低规划时间，为可扩展、可适配的机器人装配与拆解规划提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Assembly Sequence Planning (ASP) remains a challenging problem due to its combinatorial nature, making exhaustive planning approaches impractical for complex industrial assemblies. Furthermore, many CAD models lack reliable semantic contact information or require extensive manual preprocessing, limiting the applicability of existing methods. This paper presents a hybrid ASP framework combining learning-based relation extraction with geometric-symbolic reasoning to generate feasible robotic disassembly sequences from imperfect CAD data. A neural network predicts semantic geometric relations from point clouds, while human-in-the-loop verification enables correction of uncertain predictions and planning failures. Extracted relations are transformed into a symbolic assembly graph, enabling a geometric-symbolic planner to efficiently compute locally valid sets of robotic manipulation primitives. A visibility-based ray-casting strategy guides the search for feasible disassembly directions without requiring an exhaustive combinatorial search, while the local solution space enables efficient sequence optimization. The framework is evaluated on an introduced assembly dataset and on the ASAP test dataset. On the ASAP test dataset, the proposed planner achieves an 85.83% planning success rate while reducing the median planning time by more than one order of magnitude across all assembly sizes and by more than a factor of 50 for assemblies with more than 30 components compared to the baseline. The results demonstrate that the proposed hybrid framework enables efficient robotic assembly sequence planning from imperfect CAD data while substantially reducing planning time. By combining learning-based feature segmentation, human-in-the-loop verification, and geometric-symbolic reasoning, the framework provides a practical foundation for scalable and adaptable robotic assembly and disassembly planning.

</details>


### [147] [Escape-Aware Control Barrier Functions for Quadrotor Safety under Body-Rate Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17292)
*Lei Shi,Haosong Wen,Qichao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文揭示输入受限系统中仅依赖状态的CBF存在"认证缺口"，提出基于状态与上一步输入增广对的escape barrier，并将其嵌入预测控制器，在13状态四旋翼上550对闭环实验全部成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输入受限系统的CBF通常把容许输入集纳入安全集定义，但所得障碍函数几乎只依赖状态。对四旋翼而言这并非表面问题：推力矢量必须先重定向才能减速，而重定向受可达体速率限制，因此仅状态的障碍函数会认证那些实际上无法及时逃离的状态，形成"认证缺口"。

Method: 以闭式形式刻画认证缺口宽度（正比于接近速度、反比于体速率上限）；在状态与上一步输入的增广对上定义escape barrier，用一步可达推力上限衡量"逃逸权限"；给出闭式解及最大可认证接近速度的解析逆；将该障碍嵌入预测控制器且不增加额外状态开销。

Result: 550对闭环四旋翼实验中所提控制器完成全部场景；同视界的stopping-distance barrier恰好在两个落入预测缺口的场景中失败15%和25%的回合；相比在线backup-CBF基线，方向裕度大29–74度、间隙大3–18倍；独立保守rollout裁判未找到任何逃逸失败的认证状态。

Conclusion: 将输入历史纳入障碍函数可消除状态-only CBF的认证缺口，且无需额外状态代价即可嵌入预测控制，为输入受限安全关键系统提供更紧的安全认证。

Abstract: Control barrier functions for input-constrained systems place the admissible input set inside the definition of the safe set, yet the resulting barrier is almost always a function of the state alone; On a quadrotor this is not cosmetic: because the thrust vector must be reoriented before it can decelerate an approach, and reorientation is limited by the attainable body rate, a state-only barrier certifies states from which no escape is reachable in time; We characterize the certification gap in closed form and show its width is proportional to closing speed and inversely proportional to the body-rate limit; We then define an escape barrier on the augmented pair of state and previously applied input, with escape authority measured over the one-step reachable thrust cap; It admits a closed form and an analytic inverse for the maximum certifiable closing speed, and embeds in a predictive controller at no additional state cost; Across 550 paired closed-loop episodes on a 13-state quadrotor, the proposed controller completes every tested scenario, whereas the stopping-distance barrier enforced over the same horizon fails 15% and 25% of episodes in exactly the two scenarios that enter the predicted gap; Against an online backup-CBF baseline enforcing the same escape condition at the reached state, it holds a 29-74 degree larger directional margin and 3-18 times the clearance, and an independent conservative rollout referee finds no certified state from which escape fails.

</details>


### [148] [Online Geometric Change Detection via Scene Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17302)
*David Thorne,Samuel Jia Cong Chua,Nakul Joshi,Aiden Wong,Christa S. Robison,Philip Osteen,Brett T. Lopez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CDSD框架，将已建图环境空间分解为场景，通过局部子图比对实现高效在线的几何变化检测，支持单/多会话LiDAR与RGB-D建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人在长时、动态环境中执行任务时需在线识别环境变化（如倒树、开门）以支持重规划；但现有密集SLAM地图的全图比对计算代价高，无法支持单会话在线检测。

Method: CDSD将全局地图空间分解为若干唯一场景，在每个场景内提取致密且具代表性的子图，仅在局部子图子集间比对以提升效率；针对四个核心挑战给出方案：1）筛选信息冗余最小的合适场景；2）生成致密、具代表性的子图；3）处理视场差异下的子图间变化检测；4）将检测结果用于实时地图更新。

Result: 在自采的Maryland Graces Quarters陆军研究实验室设施数据集及开源多会话变化检测数据集上验证了算法有效性，可实现高效、准确的在线几何变化检测。

Conclusion: 作为首个基于子图的几何变化检测方法，CDSD在精度与计算效率间取得平衡，适用于单会话在线场景，为动态环境下的机器人长期建图与在线规划提供支持。

Abstract: Autonomous robots are increasingly deployed on long duration single- and multi-session missions in dynamic environments, where the ability to identify environmental changes such as fallen trees or opened doors provides important contextual information for online planning. We propose a framework called Change Detection via Scene Decomposition (CDSD) for accurate online geometric change detection using LiDAR or RGB-D sensors. Recent advances in geometric SLAM have made it possible to generate dense, tightly aligned maps without post processing, but comparing global maps across entire sessions is computationally expensive and does not allow for single-session online change detection. CDSD instead spatially decomposes mapped environments into unique scenes where changes can be found efficiently by comparing dense, local subsets of the global map called submaps. As the first submap-based approach for geometric change detection, we identify and address the following core challenges: 1) identifying appropriate scenes for change detection that require minimal redundant information; 2) generating dense and representative submaps for each scene; 3) detecting changes between submaps with differing fields of view; and 4) processing detected changes for real-time map reconstruction. Results demonstrate our algorithm on custom datasets collected at the Army Research Laboratory facility in Graces Quarters, Maryland, and on open-source multi-session change detection datasets.

</details>


### [149] [XPACE: Joint World and Action Modeling from Heterogeneous Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17372)
*Jiacheng Wei,Jerry Bai,Xiaoyu Yue,Zidong Wang,Xiaoyang Guo,Cheng Chen,Fanqi Pu,Fan Wu,Zhixu Yue,Yizhuo Li,Feng Qiu,Bo Liu,Yuying Ge,Hui Zhou,Chenyi Chen,Yixiao Ge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: XPACE 是一个统一的具身世界模型，同时作为世界动作模型（联合预测机器人动作与未来视频）和世界模拟器（预测指定动作的视觉后果）。其核心洞见是视频预测既能连接异构经验与动作学习，又能生成新经验用于策略改进。通过视频骨干共享、动作无标注视频学习视觉动态、动作标注的人-机器人演示联合学习，并采用粗到细课程逐步侧重机器人控制，还利用自身模拟器合成偏差-恢复轨迹进行策略自改进。在 XPENG IRON 人形机器人上验证了异构训练提升鲁棒性、人类技能迁移与真实任务完成度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人需利用多样经验、选择动作并预判动作后果。现有方法难以将异构视频经验、人类演示与机器人动作学习统一起来，也缺乏利用模型自身模拟能力生成新训练经验以实现策略自我改进的机制。

Method: 提出 XPACE 统一具身世界模型：共享视频骨干同时承担策略与世界模拟器；用无动作标注视频学视觉动态、用有动作标注的人与机器人演示联合学视频与动作预测；粗到细课程逐步强调机器人控制并保留人类经验；将模拟器适配到自生成上下文，围绕专家演示合成偏差-恢复轨迹，筛选后微调策略。

Result: 在 XPENG IRON 人形机器人上，异构训练提升鲁棒性并使人类观察到的技能迁移到机器人演示中不存在的任务；由模型自身模拟器生成的恢复数据进一步提升真实世界任务完成率。

Conclusion: 联合世界建模与动作建模能够把异构经验学习与模拟驱动的策略自我改进连接起来，为通用机器人提供可扩展的统一学习范式。

Abstract: A general-purpose robot needs to draw on diverse experience, choose actions, and anticipate how those actions will change the world. We introduce XPACE, a unified embodied world model that serves as both a world action model, jointly predicting executable robot actions and future video, and a world simulator, predicting the visual consequences of prescribed actions. Our key insight is that video prediction can both connect heterogeneous experience to action learning and generate new experience for policy improvement. With a shared video backbone between the policy and simulator, we use action-unlabeled video to learn visual dynamics and action-labeled human and robot demonstrations to jointly learn video and action prediction. Building on this architecture, a coarse-to-fine training curriculum progressively emphasizes robot control while retaining human experience, allowing the policy to learn behaviors beyond those covered by robot demonstrations. Beyond learning from recorded experience, XPACE uses its simulator to create additional recovery supervision for the policy. Specifically, we adapt the simulator to its own generated context, synthesize deviation-recovery trajectories around expert demonstrations, and fine-tune the policy on filtered recovery examples. Experiments on XPENG's IRON humanoid robot show that heterogeneous training improves robustness and enables transfer of human-observed skills to tasks absent from robot demonstrations, while recovery data generated by the model's own simulator further improves real-world task completion. Together, these results demonstrate how joint world and action modeling connects learning from heterogeneous experience with simulation-driven policy self-improvement.

</details>


### [150] [Exact Fusion and Coordinated Exploration in Multi-Robot Active Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17384)
*Peng Wu,Mohsen Imani,Amidu Kamara,Md Tamzeed Islam,Seyede Fatemeh Ghoreishi,Mahdi Imani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文指出多机器人共享环境模型学习中存在两处"共同先验重复计数"的错误：融合时局部后验相乘使共同先验被计n次，规划时各机器人以同一信念分别评分导致团队收敛到同一未知量。作者提出在共享自然参数上累加证据增量（融合时为实际增量，规划时为期望增量）来修正，并给出理论基础与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人团队通过交换信念摘要、以期望信息增益进行规划，可在共轭指数族下高效协作；但共享信念在融合与规划两个环节被重复计数，导致探索冗余与规划次优，需要形式化修正。

Method: 在共轭指数族框架下，将证据增量加到共享自然参数上：融合阶段使用各机器人的实际增量，规划阶段使用期望增量。据此定义"承诺队友的期望增量"作为下一机器人的条件增益；证明修正后的增益之和等于联合增益，去除的冗余等于规划观测流的总相关（total correlation），并保持顺序承诺下的1/2贪心保证。对高斯信念（固定采样路径）与离散主动推断新颖性近似下的Dirichlet信念给出精确/闭式凹形式；对有限假设类指出方法失效并以短枚举替代。

Result: 理论上：修正增益可加、冗余等于总相关、顺序承诺保持1/2贪心界；Dirichlet情形下团队目标有闭式凹形式且与精确互信息有显式界。实验上：在合作RockSample、觅食与野外监测任务中，融合修正不改变探索冗余，期望证据消除冗余，顺序承诺在团队规模线性代价下恢复大部分集中式联合规划的价值。

Conclusion: 共同先验的重复计数是多机器人信念共享中的关键但可修正的缺陷；以期望证据增量替换共享信念评分，可实现接近集中式联合规划的分布式协作，代价随团队规模线性增长，但对有限假设类需特殊处理。

Abstract: Robot teams that learn a common environment model exchange belief summaries and plan by the expected information gain of their actions. Under conjugate exponential-family beliefs the shared belief is counted once per robot at two points: at fusion, the product of local posteriors counts the common prior $n$ times, and at planning, every robot scores its plan under the same belief and the team converges on the same unknown. Both errors are removed by adding evidence increments to the shared natural parameter, realized increments at fusion and expected increments at planning. The expected increment of a committed teammate gives the next robot its conditional gain; corrected gains sum to the joint gain, the redundancy removed equals the total correlation of the planned observation streams, and sequential commitment keeps the $1/2$ greedy guarantee. The expected increment is exact for Gaussian beliefs with fixed sampling paths and for Dirichlet beliefs under the novelty approximation of discrete active inference, whose team objective has a closed concave form within an explicit bound of the exact mutual information, and fails for finite hypothesis classes, where a short exact enumeration replaces it. Experiments on cooperative RockSample, foraging, and field monitoring show that fusion correction leaves exploration redundancy unchanged, anticipated evidence removes it, and sequential commitment recovers most of the value of centralized joint planning at cost linear in the team size.

</details>


### [151] [Residual Fault Adaptation for Dexterous In-Hand Manipulation Under Runtime Joint Faults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17404)
*Linan Deng,Xing Liu,Lin Hong,Feng Hua,Guijun Ma,Zuogong Yue,Fumin Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出残差故障自适应（RFA）框架：以冻结的健康教师策略为锚点，训练循环残差策略从本体感受与指令响应历史中推断修正动作，配合故障注入域随机化（FIDR）与自适应采样，补偿隐藏的命令通道故障，实现灵巧手在混合故障下的操作性能提升与真机零样本部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手操作需多关节协同控制，运行时关节故障会突然破坏接触构型，导致操作失败。部署时控制器既拿不到故障标签也没有切换信号，因此需要一种能隐式推断并补偿隐藏命令通道故障的方法。

Method: RFA 保留冻结的健康教师提供标称行为，训练循环（recurrent）残差策略，仅凭本体感受与指令响应历史输出修正动作。训练阶段用故障注入域随机化（FIDR）随机化故障模式、受影响关节、严重程度与发生时刻；自适应采样提高近期表现较差的故障模式采样频率；另用冻结的 Direct FIDR 策略仅在故障激活的训练样本上提供分布参考，部署时移除。部署控制器不接收故障标签或切换信号。

Result: 仿真灵巧手实验：在固定混合故障协议下，RFA 相对健康策略提升操作性能；真机实验中通过软件注入故障，验证了所学自适应策略的零样本部署。

Conclusion: 基于教师锚定的残差学习加故障注入域随机化，可在无故障标签、无切换信号条件下隐式补偿隐藏命令通道故障，并具备从仿真到真机的零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: Dexterous in-hand manipulation requires coordinated control of multiple actuated joints, and a runtime joint fault can abruptly disrupt the contact configuration required for successful manipulation. In this work, we propose residual fault adaptation (RFA), a teacher-anchored framework for compensating for hidden command-channel faults. RFA retains a frozen healthy teacher to provide nominal behavior and trains a recurrent residual policy to infer corrective actions from proprioceptive and command-response history. During training, fault-injection domain randomization (FIDR) varies the fault mode, affected joint, severity, and onset time, while adaptive sampling increases the frequency of fault modes associated with lower recent performance. A frozen Direct FIDR policy provides a distributional reference only on fault-active training samples and is absent from deployment. The deployed controller receives neither fault labels nor controller-switching signals. Simulation experiments on the dexterous hand indicate that RFA can improve manipulation performance relative to the healthy policy under a fixed mixed-fault protocol. Real-robot experiments with software-injected faults further demonstrate zero-shot deployment of the learned adaptation policy.

</details>


### [152] [Optimized Wrench Polytope Analysis for Real-Time Stability Control of Legged Robots in Complex Multi-Contact Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17405)
*Friedrich Graaf,Elias Birkefeld,Christian Eichmann,Elias Hofele,Tristan Schnell,Georg Heppner,Arne Roennau,Rüdiger Dillmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种优化算法快速计算任意接触场景下的全可驱动扳量多面体，使各关节扭矩可在49 Hz控制频率内解算，并在仿真与真实步行机器人上验证了稳定性控制器的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斜坡、洞穴、脚手架等难以通行的地形对足式机器人仍是重大挑战，现有控制器难以在这些复杂接触场景中保证稳定，且缺乏可在常规控制回路频率下实时评估接触力/扭矩多面体的算法。

Method: 提出针对任意接触场景计算"全可驱动扳量多面体"（full actuatable wrench polytope）的优化算法，通过改进分析算法加速多面体评估，使机器人各关节扭矩可在49 Hz控制频率内完成计算，从而可嵌入常规控制回路中，实现对不同接触场景下机器人位姿的驱动控制。

Result: 在多种仿真场景中对稳定性控制器进行了广泛评估，并部署于真实步行机器人硬件上验证其适用性；控制器在目前已公开的其他控制器无法实现的复杂场景中实现了稳定。

Conclusion: 该算法实现了可实时嵌入控制回路的接触扳量多面体评估，所提稳定性控制器兼具仿真与真实硬件验证，可在高难度接触场景下提供稳定控制。

Abstract: Legged robots offer a variety of automation applications in real-world scenarios. But areas that are difficult to traverse, like slopes, caves, or scaffolding, still pose a great challenge for traversal. To tackle this problem, we propose an optimized algorithm for evaluating the full actuatable wrench polytope for arbitrary contact scenarios. With our improved analysis algorithm, the torques for each joint of the robot can be calculated within a control frequency of 49 Hz. The achieved speedup allows for deployment within a regular control loop for actuating robot poses for different contact scenarios. We evaluated our stability controller extensively in simulation scenarios and validated its applicability by deploying it on actual walking robot hardware. The proposed controller achieved stability in very complex scenarios that are currently not achievable by any other controller.

</details>


### [153] [Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability for Hybrid Systems: Unified Goal-Driven Control with Safety Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17430)
*Javier Borquez,Shuang Peng,Somil Bansal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将经典 Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) 可达性分析扩展到混合动力系统，定义了覆盖离散与连续状态的广义值函数以刻画安全集，并提出数值求解算法、混合最小限制安全滤波器以及混合后向可达-规避管（reach-avoid tube），在四足机器人仿真与实物实验中验证了混合模式规划与安全关键任务的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合动力系统是接触丰富环境下机器人建模的有力框架，但连续动力学与离散模式切换的强耦合使得安全性与性能保证十分困难。经典 HJ 可达性分析仅适用于连续时间非线性系统，无法直接处理离散跳变与模式切换带来的复杂性，因此需要将其推广到混合系统。

Method: （1）将 HJ 可达性分析推广到混合动力系统，构造在离散状态与连续状态上同时定义的广义值函数，并纳入控制约束与模型不确定性；（2）给出该值函数的数值计算算法；（3）基于安全集提出混合最小限制安全滤波器，仅在必要时同时干预标称控制器的离散与连续分量；（4）提出并计算混合后向可达-规避管，同时保证安全与到达目标。

Result: 在仿真研究与四足机器人真实实验中验证了框架，展示了其在混合模式规划与安全关键应用中的有效性；安全滤波器可在避免不安全状态的前提下尽量保留标称行为，可达-规避管可合成同时保证安全与任务完成的连续与离散控制策略。

Conclusion: 该工作将形式化验证方法（HJ 可达性）成功扩展到混合系统，为接触丰富机器人任务提供了兼顾安全性、性能与目标到达的统一框架，并具备实物可行性。

Abstract: Hybrid dynamical systems provide a powerful modeling framework for robotic systems, particularly in contact-rich environments. However, ensuring safety and performance in such systems remains challenging due to the intricate coupling between continuous dynamics and discrete mode transitions. In this work, we extend classical Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) reachability analysis, a formal verification method for continuous-time nonlinear systems, to hybrid dynamical systems. Our framework characterizes safe sets for hybrid systems through a generalized value function defined over both discrete and continuous states while accounting for control constraints and model uncertainty. We additionally provide a numerical algorithm to compute this value function. Building on these safe sets, we propose two different mechanisms to integrate performance objectives. First, we introduce a hybrid least-restrictive safety filter that intervenes on both the discrete and continuous components of a nominal controller only when necessary to avoid unsafe states, thereby preserving nominal behavior whenever possible. Second, we formulate and compute hybrid backward reach-avoid tubes, enabling the simultaneous enforcement of safety and goal-reaching behavior, an extension not previously addressed within hybrid HJ reachability. This enables the synthesis of continuous and discrete control policies that guarantee both safety and task completion. We validate our framework through simulation studies and real-world experiments on a quadrupedal robot, demonstrating its effectiveness in hybrid mode planning and safety-critical applications.

</details>


### [154] [Gaussian Processes for Modelling Spatial Fields with Robot Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17463)
*Guillermo Legarda Herranz,Gianpiero Francesca,Mauro Birattari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出位置无关高斯过程回归(LU-GPR)，使无外部定位的机器人集群仅靠局部感知与通信即可协同建模空间场，并在线收敛到统一坐标系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人集群空间场建模方法依赖外部定位系统(如GPS/动捕)为每个机器人提供自身位置，限制了在无定位环境(水下、灾后、室内)下的可扩展性与鲁棒性。作者希望去除这一依赖。

Method: LU-GPR：每个机器人用GPR推断空间场的后验均值与方差，同时通过局部感知与通信与同伴迭代对齐到共同参考系；提出在线算法使局部参考系收敛时局部估计保持一致；再通过product of experts将邻居估计与自身估计融合为全局模型。

Result: 实验表明LU-GPR随机器人数量具有良好可扩展性，并对有限通信范围具有鲁棒性；在真实场景中用于估计疏散人群流场，验证了实际可行性。

Conclusion: 无外部定位条件下的分布式空间场建模可行，为集群在定位受限环境中的场估计与协同感知提供了可扩展、鲁棒的方案。

Abstract: Robot swarms, by virtue of their decentralised architecture, are a natural tool for scalable, robust modelling of spatial fields, such as water temperature, wind velocity, or terrain elevation. However, existing methods rely on external positioning systems that allow each robot to determine its own position in space. Here, we introduce location-unaware Gaussian process regression (LU-GPR) as a solution to the modelling of spatial fields in the absence of such positioning systems. LU-GPR allows each robot to infer the posterior mean and variance of the field in space, while simultaneously agreeing on a common frame of reference with its peers, using only local sensing and communication. We propose an online algorithm that allows each robot to consistently infer local estimates as its local frame of reference converges to the common one. By means of a product of experts model, each robot also combines the estimates of its peers with its own to obtain a global model. Our results show that LU-GPR scales well with the number of robots and is robust to limited communication ranges. We also demonstrate how it can be used in real-world monitoring scenarios to estimate the flow of an evacuating crowd.

</details>


### [155] [Dissecting Motion-Prior Regularization for Data-Scarce Robotic Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17484)
*Ning Hu,Shuai Li,Jindong Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在仅有15条演示的扩散策略上，比较最小急动、速度-曲率及联合运动先验正则对真实机器人插入成功率的影响（每设置80次试验）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究小样本（少演示）条件下，训练期运动先验正则化能否提升扩散策略的插入任务成功率，并检验不同先验机制是否带来增益或协同。

Method: 以扩散策略为基座，仅用15条演示训练；对比最小急动（抑制平移加速度突变）、速度-曲率耦合（速度与路径几何关联）、二者联合、无先验、通用平滑五种设置；每设置80次真实机器人试验，跨四类记录条件汇总；用成功率与Wilson 95%置信区间可视化比较。

Result: 联合与仅最小急动均为70/80（87.5%）；仅速度-曲率69/80（86.3%）；无先验66/80（82.5%）；通用平滑67/80（83.8%）。联合比无先验高5.0个百分点，但相对仅最小急动无观测增益。

Conclusion: 最小急动是更简单、可复现的候选机制；未证实先验间协同、生物力学特异性、安全性提升或分布偏移鲁棒性。

Abstract: This study asks whether training-time motion-prior regularization can improve insertion success when a diffusion policy is learned from only 15 demonstrations. Minimum jerk discourages abrupt changes in predicted translational acceleration; speed-curvature regularization instead couples movement speed to path geometry. These are candidate mechanisms for task completion, not safety guarantees. We compare the priors individually and jointly, neither prior, and generic smoothness, with 80 real-robot trials per setting pooled over four recorded condition classes. Joint and minimum-jerk-only settings each achieved 70/80 successes (87.5%), versus 69/80 (86.3%) for speed-curvature only, 66/80 (82.5%) for neither prior, and 67/80 (83.8%) for generic smoothness. Success rates and Wilson 95% confidence intervals are visualized for direct comparison. Joint regularization exceeded neither by 5.0 percentage points but provided no observed gain over minimum jerk alone. The results motivate minimum jerk as the simpler candidate for replication, without establishing synergy, biomechanical specificity, improved safety, or distribution-shift robustness.

</details>


### [156] [Modality-Autoregressive World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17524)
*Adam Hung,Bardienus P. Duisterhof,Deva Ramanan,Jeffrey Ichnowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ModAR 是首个在预测动作前自回归地去噪多个未来模态（如点轨迹、DINO 特征、深度）的世界-动作模型，系统性研究了训练数据混合、预测模态与 WAM 公式对性能的影响，在真实双臂任务上以约 1/20 训练 FLOPs 且无需预训练即取得 75% 平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAM）通常以 RGB 图像预测未来，但深度、预训练视觉特征、点轨迹等视觉模态能更高效地捕获几何、语义与运动信息；如何在 WAM 中最佳组合这些模态仍是未解问题。

Method: 提出 ModAR：在预测动作前自回归地依次去噪多个未来模态，使每次预测可条件于此前生成的模态；并从零训练，系统性消融训练数据混合、预测模态组合及 WAM 公式。

Result: WAM 从预测点轨迹、DINO 特征与深度图中获益，额外预测未来 RGB 无一致收益；ModAR 的顺序生成在所有数据规模下均优于现有 WAM 公式。与视频模型初始化的 Flex-π 相比，ModAR 平均成功率达 75%（对比 72%），训练 FLOPs 约少 20 倍且无需预训练；在三个真实双臂任务上优于基线，并能受益于人类视频。

Conclusion: 多模态自回归去噪的未来建模是构建 WAM 的有效范式，点轨迹/深度/DINO 特征比 RGB 更具预测价值，且无需大规模视频预训练即可在真实机器人任务上取得领先表现。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) jointly model future observations and actions, typically predicting the future as RGB images. Other visual modalities such as depth, pretrained visual features, and point tracks can more efficiently capture geometric, semantic, and motion features. However, how best to combine these modalities within WAMs remains an open question. We introduce ModAR, the first WAM to autoregressively denoise multiple future modalities before predicting actions. This allows each prediction to condition on previously generated modalities. We train from scratch to systematically study how training-data mixtures, predicted modalities, and WAM formulations affect performance. In our evaluations, WAMs benefit from predicting point tracks, DINO features, and depth maps, while additionally predicting future RGB does not provide a consistent benefit. We also find that ModAR's sequential generation outperforms existing WAM formulations, with the highest average success rate at all evaluated data scales. We also fine-tune the video-model-initialized WAM Flex-$\pi$ on the same data; ModAR achieves a slightly higher observed average success rate (75% vs. 72%) while using approximately $20\times$ fewer training FLOPs and no pretraining. On three real-world bimanual tasks, ModAR outperforms baselines and improves with human videos.

</details>
